Abstract: A resonant classical field drives a two-level system coherently and, with ordinary relaxation, produces saturation rather than directional population transfer. Here we show that directionality emerges when the excitation source is retained as a quantum subsystem and subsequently traced out. We model the source as a single-excitation degree of freedom, or equivalently a stream of two-level ancillas, exchange-coupled to a target two-level system. The source first moment generates the usual coherent drive, while its second moments produce upward and downward dissipative channels. For a number-diagonal, fully excited source, the first moment vanishes and only the upward channel remains, yielding monotonic pumping of the target. The same structure follows from both an exact collision map and a fluctuation-regulated second-order quantum master equation. For exponentially decaying source correlations, the pumping rate shows a Lorentzian detuning dependence governed by the correlation time $τ_c$, accompanied by a dispersive frequency shift. In the presence of thermal relaxation, the steady-state excited-state population can exceed the resonantly driven optical-Bloch limit of one half. Within the fluctuation-regulated master equation, this directional pumping arises as a drive-induced dissipative contribution whose strength and spectral response are controlled by $τ_c$.

</details>


### [82] [Classical simulation of amplitude-damped bucket-brigade quantum random access memories via predictable branch evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34935)
*Zhao-Yun Chen,Ming-Yang Tan,Sheng Zhang,Peng Wang,Yun-Jie Wang,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了bucket-brigade QRAM在振幅阻尼噪声下分支剪枝模拟的完整可预测性理论，覆盖qutrit与qubit两种编码，并据此设计了只演化坏分支加一条参考分支的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟含噪QRAM时，分支剪枝可大幅加速：只采样噪声轨迹，对避开所有故障的“好分支”无需演化，因其末态可解析预测。但在振幅阻尼下，无跳变算符K0在每个时间片作用于每个分支，使这种可预测性不再自动成立，需专门刻画。

Method: 分别分析两种编码：qutrit编码中K0在节点基下对角，等待态|W⟩为其不动点，好分支沿无噪轨道演化至多差一个标量衰减；qubit编码中两个Hadamard墙之间的相位回踢式读取使K0非对角，但结构可闭式精确求解。据此修正qubit编码的剪枝判据。

Result: 在无跳变条件下，好分支的分支内不保真度为阻尼率γ的二阶量，每数据比特的相干泄漏约为n²γ²/4，闭式解无近似地刻画该量；轨迹级测试表明所有闭式结果达到机器精度。

Conclusion: 所得算法仅演化坏分支加一条参考分支，其余开销与被替代的完整演化一致；端到端基准显示剪枝相对完整演化的加速比最高达285倍。

Abstract: Classical simulation of quantum random access memory (QRAM) under noise can be accelerated dramatically by branch pruning: noise histories are sampled as trajectory ensembles, and good branches, whose routing paths avoid every sampled fault, need not be evolved because their final states are analytically predictable. Under amplitude damping this predictability is not automatic, because the no-jump operator $K_0$ acts on every branch at every time slice. Here we give a complete account of damping-channel predictability in bucket-brigade QRAM simulation, for both qutrit and qubit encodings. For the qutrit encoding, $K_0$ is diagonal in the node basis, and the wait state $|W\rangle$ is its fixed point, so a good branch follows the noiseless orbit up to a scalar attenuation. In the qubit encoding, phase-kickback memory fetching between two Hadamard walls makes $K_0$ no longer diagonal, yet the resulting structure is exactly solvable in closed form. Conditional on no jump, the in-branch infidelity of a good branch is second order in the damping rate $γ$, with coherent leakage $\sim n^2γ^2/4$ per data qubit, and the closed form captures it without approximation. We also revise the pruning criterion for the qubit encoding. The resulting algorithm evolves only the bad branches plus one reference branch and shares every other cost with the full evolution it replaces. It is validated by end-to-end benchmarks with pruned-over-full speedups up to $285\times$ and by trajectory-level tests confirming every closed form to machine precision.

</details>


### [83] [All-vs-Nothing Operational Manifestation of Preparation Contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34964)
*Jayashree Karmakar,Rafiuddin Gazi,Biswadeep Chatterjee,Subhendu B Ghosh,Anandamay Das Bhowmik,Ananya Chakraborty,Manik Banik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出奇偶盲隐藏匹配任务，实现制备上下文性的“全有或全无”分离：量子协议以单条⌈log₂n⌉量子比特消息完美成功，而任何制备非上下文模型都无法对偶数n≥6做到完美成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往制备上下文性多表现为信息处理任务中相对制备非上下文模型的定量优势，量子理论通常无法达到完美成功；作者希望构造一种定性的、全有或全无的制备上下文性体现。

Method: 引入奇偶盲隐藏匹配任务：Alice将n比特串编码为不泄露除两比特奇偶性以外任何输入奇偶性的消息；Bob给定输入位置上的完美匹配，须输出匹配中的一条边及其两端比特的奇偶性。证明任意制备非上下文模型在偶数n≥6时不能完美成功，结论适用于任意本体状态空间，且不假设结果确定性或测量非上下文性。渐近上界由布尔超立方体上超压缩性导出的Fourier分析求和规则得到。

Result: 量子协议使用单条⌈log₂n⌉量子比特消息即可满足奇偶盲性并以确定性成功。n=6和n=8时最优非上下文成功概率分别为4/5和3/4；渐近最优非上下文成功概率为1/2+Θ(1/√n)。n=8时即使Bob仅限使用105个完美匹配中合适的4个，非上下文界仍为3/4。

Conclusion: 该分离是定性而非仅定量的：量子理论实现完美成功，制备非上下文模型无法做到。n=8的限制条件使效应进入实验可达范围。

Abstract: Preparation contextuality is often manifested operationally through a quantitative advantage over preparation-noncontextual models in information-processing tasks, yet quantum theory typically falls short of perfect success. Here we introduce a parity-oblivious Hidden Matching task that instead yields an all-vs-nothing manifestation of preparation contextuality. Alice encodes an $n$-bit string so that her message reveals no information about any input parity other than the two-bit parities. Bob, given a perfect matching on the input positions, must output an edge of the matching together with the parity of its two bits. A quantum protocol using a single $\lceil\log_2 n\rceil$-qubit message satisfies the parity-obliviousness constraint and succeeds with certainty. We prove that no preparation-noncontextual model achieves perfect success for any even $n\ge6$. The result holds for arbitrary ontic state spaces and assumes neither outcome determinism nor measurement noncontextuality. For $n=6$ and $n=8$, the optimal noncontextual success probabilities are $4/5$ and $3/4$, respectively, while asymptotically the optimal noncontextual success probability is $1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. Our asymptotic upper bound follows from a Fourier-analytic sum rule derived via hypercontractivity on the Boolean hypercube. The resulting separation is qualitative rather than merely quantitative: quantum theory achieves perfect success, whereas preparation-noncontextual models cannot. We aslo show that at $n=8$ the noncontextual bound remains $3/4$ even when Bob is restricted to just suitable $4$ of the $105$ possible perfect matchings, bringing the effect within experimental reach.

</details>


### [84] [Simulation-Based Quantum System Inference with Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34995)
*Hang Zou,Anton Frisk Kockum,Martin Rahm,Simon Olsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无似然的量子系统推断框架，用经典模拟器配对神经密度估计器，一次性训练即可从测量记录直接输出参数后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统从测量数据反推参数面临根本挑战：Hilbert空间指数级增长导致似然函数计算不可行。传统逐实验推断成本高、需大量数据采集。

Method: 仿真驱动的量子系统推断：将多项式代价的经典模拟器（如Pauli传播、张量网络）与归一化流或其他神经密度估计器配对，构建统一的无似然框架，直接从经典模拟数据学习参数后验。单次训练后的模型将任意新测量记录通过一次前向传播映射为后验。

Result: 在Pauli噪声学习、量子误差缓解、量子态层析和哈密顿量学习上验证框架的通用性；示例涵盖81比特浅层电路和735参数推断。各案例均准确估计可辨识参数，后验不确定性作为不可辨识性诊断，并指示需要进一步表征之处。

Conclusion: 该框架降低量子实验的数据采集需求并加速参数推断，为大规模量子系统的表征与改进提供实用途径。

Abstract: Models of quantum systems faithfully map system parameters to observations, but the inverse problem of parameter inference from measurement data presents a fundamental challenge: computationally intractable likelihoods due to an exponentially large Hilbert space. Here, we introduce simulation-based quantum system inference, a unified, likelihood-free framework that learns parameter posteriors directly from classical simulation data. The central idea is to pair polynomial-cost classical simulators, such as Pauli propagation and tensor networks, with normalizing flows or other neural density estimators for accurate, reusable inference. A single model, trained once, maps any new measurement record to its posterior in one forward pass---turning per-experiment inference into a fixed, up-front cost. We numerically demonstrate the framework's versatility across Pauli noise learning, quantum error mitigation, quantum state tomography, and Hamiltonian learning, with examples involving 81-qubit shallow circuits and 735-parameter inference. In each case, the approach yields accurate estimates of identifiable parameters, while posterior uncertainty provides additional diagnostics of non-identifiability and indicates where further characterization is needed. Our framework reduces data-acquisition requirements in quantum experiments and accelerates parameter inference, providing a practical route to characterizing and improving large-scale quantum systems.

</details>


### [85] [Cyclotomic Cosets: Hidden Subgroup and Quantum Sieving Algorithm for Prime-Power Moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34996)
*Mathias Boucher,Pierre-Alain Fouque,Yixin Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分圆陪集问题（CCP），作为DCP的分圆推广，保持了精确的隐藏子群结构；利用π进理想链构造量子筛，在准多项式时间与多项式量子空间内求解CCP、均匀EDCP及高斯S|LWE>，但不直接给出标准LWE的准多项式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE是后量子密码的基础假设。Regev给出了LWE到二面体陪集问题（DCP）的量子归约；Brakerski等人提出的外推二面体陪集问题（EDCP）与LWE等价，但EDCP不再具有陪集结构，限制了隐藏子群问题技术的直接应用。因此需要一种既保留精确隐藏子群结构、又足以刻画LWE相关困难性的推广形式。

Method: 引入分圆陪集问题（CCP）：设ζ_p为本原p次单位根，π=ζ_p−1，q=p^t，L=t(p−1)，在R_q=Z_q[ζ_p]≅Z[ζ_p]/(π^L)上工作，该同构来自完全分歧恒等式(p)=(π)^{p-1}。利用由此产生的π进理想链构造量子筛，将相位态依次模π^L, π^{L−1}, …, π 逐步归约。

Result: 对每个固定素数p与模数q=p^t，算法以时间和样本复杂度2^{O_p(log n log q)}求解CCP，且仅使用多项式量子空间。该筛同样适用于均匀EDCP与高斯S|LWE>，当q=poly(n)时对这些问题的求解达到准多项式时间。该结果将Bai等人（CRYPTO 2025）的2的幂次EDCP筛推广至分圆情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了具有精确隐藏子群结构的分圆陪集问题及其高效量子筛求解方法，实现了EDCP与S|LWE>在多项式模数下的准多项式时间算法。但作者强调，该结果本身并不给出标准LWE的准多项式时间算法，因为目前已知的归约只能产生数量有限的近似CCP态。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem is a fundamental assumption in post-quantum cryptography. Regev established a quantum reduction from LWE to the Dihedral Coset Problem (DCP). Later, Brakerski et al. introduced the Extrapolated Dihedral Coset Problem (EDCP), proving its equivalence to LWE. However, unlike DCP, EDCP no longer admits a coset structure. This limits the direct application of techniques for hidden subgroup problems.
  In this work, we introduce the Cyclotomic Coset Problem (CCP), a cyclotomic generalization of DCP that preserves an exact hidden-subgroup structure. Let $ζ_p$ be a primitive $p$-th root of unity, let $π=ζ_p-1$, and write $q=p^t$ and $L=t(p-1)$. We work over $R_q=\mathbb Z_q[ζ_p] \cong \mathbb Z[ζ_p]/(π^L)$, where the isomorphism follows from the total ramification identity $(p)=(π)^{p-1}$. We exploit the resulting $π$-adic ideal chain to construct a quantum sieve that successively reduces phase states modulo $π^{L},π^{L-1},\ldots,π$. For every fixed prime $p$ and modulus $q=p^t$, our algorithm solves the CCP in time and sample complexity $2^{O_p(\log n\log q)}$, using polynomial quantum space. The sieve also applies to uniform EDCP and Gaussian S|LWE>, yielding quasi-polynomial time algorithms for all the above problems when $q=\text{poly}(n)$. This extends the power-of-two EDCP sieve of Bai et al. (CRYPTO 2025) to a cyclotomic setting.
  However, we emphasize that our result does not, by itself, yield a quasi-polynomial-time algorithm for standard LWE, because the currently known reduction produces only a limited number of approximate CCP states.

</details>


### [86] [Enantiomer-specific pumping of chiral molecules in high-$J$ rotational states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35008)
*Fen Zou,Yan Gao,Peng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种涉及 J=1 与 J=2 转动能级的手性分子对映体特异性态转移(ESST)方案，通过三束线偏振微波场与一束激光驱动对映体特异性泵浦，实现一个对映体被泵入暗态、另一个被耗散泵出该子流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ESST 实验演示主要集中于最低转动角动量态(J=0、J=1)，而实际分子样品在高 J 转动态可能存在可观布居；由于高 J 流形包含更多磁子能级与更复杂的电偶极跃迁网络，无法通过低 J 方案的简单直接推广获得适用方案，因此亟需发展适用于高 J 态的 ESST 方案。

Method: 针对手性分子，构建涉及 J=1 与 J=2 转动能级的 ESST 方案：采用三束线偏振微波场加一束激光束驱动的对映体特异性泵浦，在 J=1,2 的子流形内将某一对映体泵入对映体特异性暗态，而另一对映体则被耗散泵出该流形。

Result: 所提方案可在 J=1,2 子流形中实现一个对映体进入对映体特异性暗态、另一对映体被耗散移出该流形；该策略可推广至涉及更高角动量转动态的系统。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了 ESST 仅适用于最低转动态的局限，为高 J 转动态下手性分子的对映体检测与分离提供了可行方案，并具备向更高角动量体系扩展的能力。

Abstract: Enantiomer-specific state transfer (ESST) based on electric-dipole transitions has attracted considerable interest in the studies of manipulating molecules with electromagnetic fields. It also holds promise as a powerful tool for enantiomer detection and separation. To date, experimental demonstrations of ESST have focused mainly on molecules in the lowest rotational angular-momentum states, namely, $J=0$ and $J=1$. Realistic molecular samples, however, may contain appreciable populations in higher-$J$ rotational states, making it desirable to develop ESST schemes applicable to such states. Nevertheless, one cannot derive such schemes via a simple and straightforward extension of existing low-$J$ schemes, because higher-$J$ manifolds contain more magnetic sub-levels and involve more complicated networks of electric-dipole transitions. Here, we propose an ESST scheme for chiral molecules involving the $J=1$ and $J=2$ rotational states. Specifically, our scheme is based on enantiomer-specific pumping driven by three linearly polarized microwave fields and one laser beam. The transduction uses operator Fourier transforms and relative decoding to recover source operators rather than individual term-selection labels. The relative distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}$ is the first degree at which source and target traces disagree. Abstract: The output of a parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) can be expressed as a finite Fourier series whose accessible frequencies are fixed by the data-encoding gates. While the encoder determines which frequencies can appear, the corresponding Fourier coefficients depend on how the trainable and entangling gates are arranged. Although existing studies provide metrics for characterizing how gate structure affects Fourier coefficients, they do not translate these analyses into an explicit design criterion specifying how gates should be arranged to make a target coefficient reachable. In this paper, we provide such a criterion. Using the adjoint action of the encoding generator, we decompose operator space into two-dimensional invariant planes indexed by frequency and show that each Fourier coefficient is exactly a sum of bilinear projections of the effective state and observable onto the planes at that frequency. Because, for Pauli encodings, high-frequency planes are spanned by mixed multi-qubit Pauli strings, a target coefficient can contribute to the output only when local rotations and entangling layers are arranged so that both the effective state and observable acquire support on one of its planes. For Pauli readouts and commuting two-qubit entanglers, this yields a circuit design rule that specifies, for a given interaction graph, a placement of local rotations and entangling layers that makes a target Fourier coefficient reachable. Using a single encoding layer, we validate the proposed design through a placement ablation, regression tasks from PDEBench and physics-informed Maxwell field modeling, and further demonstrate its robustness to moderate simulated gate noise and damping.

TL;DR: 该论文提出随机二人博弈（expectiminimax 树）的量子算法，通过对叶子值的量子查询以均方根误差 ε 估计博弈价值，当深度视为常数时达到 Õ(deg^{m/2}ε^{-1})，相较经典 Õ(deg^{m}ε^{-2}) 同时获得分支因子的二次加速与精度的二次加速。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for stochastic $2$-player games. Their game trees are the expectiminimax trees of classical game search: $m$ adversarial layers alternate with $m$ chance layers, every node has $deg$ children, and the leaf values lie in an interval of length $N$. The algorithm estimates the value of the game to root mean square error $ε$ with \[ O\!\left( K^{D}\, deg^{\frac{D}{4}} \, \frac{N}ε \, D^{4D} \log\left( \frac{deg N}ε \right)^{4D} \right), \qquad D = 2m \] queries to the leaf values, where $K$ is an absolute constant. If we treat the depth $D$ as a constant as is sometimes done in the literature, the bound is $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}ε^{-1})$ against the classical $\widetilde O(deg^{m}ε^{-2})$. So the quadratic speedup in the branching factor known for adversarial trees and the quadratic speedup in the accuracy known for a single expectation both survive when we nest one inside the other.
  We use a derandomised multilevel Monte Carlo estimator for chance layers and a coherent binary search for adversarial layers. The composition is done by introducing a conversion between root-mean-square and uniform error guarantees, and a composition to nest it with quantum mean estimation. We state the algorithm for stochastic games, but it is valid for the following conditions: the value of a parent is a Lipschitz function of the values of its children and the value at a chance vertex is linear in its children, however the speedup is preserved only if the deterministic step has a quantum subroutine with sublinear query complexity.

</details>


### [95] [Induced Coherence in Quantum and Classical Interferometry: Origin and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35523)
*Balakrishnan Viswanathan,Ravi Kamal Pandey,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Sibasish Ghosh,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子诱导相干干涉仪中验证了相干性、可预测性与纠缠的普适三重关系,经典构型退化为已知的对偶不等式;并揭示路径不可区分性对干涉与纠缠的互补调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杨氏双缝干涉体现的波粒二象性满足Englert-Greenberger-Yasin不等式,近期被证明服从涉及相干性、可预测性和纠缠的普适三重关系。作者希望检验该三重关系是否在诱导相干干涉仪中依然成立,并探究相干性与纠缠的来源及调控手段。

Method: 以诱导相干干涉仪为平台,分别构造量子构型与经典构型进行对比分析;引入路径不可区分性(源的固有不可区分性)作为核心参量,分析其对干涉与纠缠的影响,并刻画两种构型中相干性的本质。

Result: 三重关系在量子诱导相干干涉仪中持续成立;其经典构型则退化为熟知的对偶不等式。路径不可区分性对干涉与纠缠均产生互补影响,并可由此调控相干性与纠缠。

Conclusion: 诱导相干干涉仪可作为检验波粒二象性三重关系的有效平台,经典与量子版本中相干性的本质得到明确刻画,源的不可区分性是统一调控干涉与纠缠的关键资源。

Abstract: The wave-particle duality that manifests in the Young's double-slit interferometry satisfying the well known Englert-Greenberger-Yasin inequality has recently been shown to obey a universal triality relation involving coherence, predictability and rather surprisingly entanglement. In the present work, we explicitly demonstrate the persistence of the triality relation in the quantum induced coherence interferometer and the classical configuration recovers the well known duality inequality. Furthermore, we explore the origin of coherence and entanglement, in the induced coherence interferometer, and means to control them. We show that path indistinguishability (or intrinsic indistinguishability of the sources) in our setup plays a complementary role in influencing both interference and entanglement. We bring out manifestly the nature of coherence in both classical and quantum versions of induced coherence interferometers.

</details>


### [96] [Analysis of asymmetric errors in NISQ experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35535)
*Gil Kalai,Tomer Shoham,Carsten Voelkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文延续对NISQ实验（尤其是Google 2019"量子霸权"实验）的分析，扩展此前关于对称读取误差的研究，转向分析非对称读取误差，结合实验数据与模拟进行考察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作arXiv:2008.05177提出分析对称与非对称读取误差的统计工具，arXiv:2404.00935针对对称情形深入发展了Fourier方法。Fefferman等（arXiv:2306.16659）指出振幅阻尼门误差会导致量子计算机样本中零的比例偏高，本文据此动机检验该效应是否存在于Google实验数据中。

Method: 扩展并补充既有研究，针对实验与模拟数据中的非对称读取误差进行分析；使用IBM和Google模拟器进行n=12,14的模拟；通过考察样本中零和一的分数来区分门误差效应与读取误差非对称性的贡献。

Result: 在Google实验数据中，对于全部量子比特数范围12≤n≤53，零和一的比例仅反映所报告读取误差的非对称性，未观察到Fefferman等所述的振幅阻尼门误差导致的零比例增加效应；并在n=12,14的IBM与Google模拟器上进行了相应模拟。

Conclusion: Google实验数据中的零/一比例可由读取误差的非对称性充分解释，而非门误差（如振幅阻尼）所致；本文的分析支持对NISQ实验中读取误差非对称性的进一步统计刻画。

Abstract: This paper continues our analysis of NISQ experiments and especially the Google 2019 ``quantum supremacy" experiment. In arXiv:2008.05177 we proposed statistical tools to analyze symmetric and asymmetric readout errors, and the symmetric case was extensively developed and studied in arXiv:2404.00935, where we used Fourier methods combined with statistical tools.
  Here we extend and complement our earlier study and analyze asymmetric readout errors for data from experiments and simulations.
  Our study was further motivated by a paper by Fefferman et al. arXiv:2306.16659, who showed that the effect of amplitude damping gate errors is to have larger fractions of zeroes in the samples produced by the quantum computer. We show that this effect of gate errors is not observed in the Google experimental data, where, for the entire range of the number of qubits, $12 \le n \le 53$, the fraction of zeroes and ones reflects only the asymmetry of reported readout errors, and report on simulations with $n=12,14$ using IBM's and Google's simulators.

</details>


### [97] [Sublinear Copies Suffice for Fidelity Estimation with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35547)
*Jayadev Acharya,Abhilash Dharmavarapu,Yuhan Liu,Nengkun Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种估计已知目标态与未知实验制备态之间量子保真度的协议，在精度 ε 下所需泡利基测量次数为次线性量级 o(d^{0.9908}/ε²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子保真度是刻画量子态制备质量的核心指标，但直接估计通常需要大量测量。现有方法在维度 d 较大时测量复杂度接近线性甚至更高，因此亟需降低测量开销。

Method: 设计一个基于泡利基测量的保真度估计协议，通过次线性（sublinear）数量的泡利基测量来推断目标态与未知制备态之间的量子保真度，控制估计误差在 ε 以内。

Result: 该协议实现精度 ε 的保真度估计，所需泡利基测量次数为 o(d^{0.9908}/ε²)，即在维度 d 上呈次线性增长（指数小于 1）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了保真度估计在测量复杂度上的次线性上界，突破了以往近线性甚至更高的测量开销限制，为高效量子态验证提供了新途径。

Abstract: We present a protocol that estimates the quantum fidelity, up to precision $\varepsilon$, between a known target state and unknown lab-prepared state with sublinear, $o(d^{0.9908}/\varepsilon^2)$, number of Pauli basis measurements.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Query Complexity Beyond the Worst Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35580)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Yanlin Chen,Amin Shiraz Gilani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典光滑分析框架引入量子算法领域，得到超越最坏情况的三项结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典光滑分析可解释算法在实际输入上的良好表现，作者希望建立其量子对应版本。

Method: 定义光滑量子查询复杂度；证明存在总函数使其光滑量子查询复杂度指数级小于经典查询复杂度；对对称布尔函数刻画光滑随机与量子查询复杂度；研究模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题；核心工具是近似计算两非重复串间碰撞数的量子算法。

Result: (1) 存在总函数其光滑量子查询复杂度指数级优于经典查询复杂度；(2) 对称布尔函数的光滑随机与量子查询复杂度获近紧刻画，统一了最坏情况与平均情况结果；(3) 字符串问题在多种区间下取得多项式至超多项式量子加速；改进 Le Gall 与 Ng 的碰撞数近似算法。

Conclusion: 光滑分析可揭示比最坏情况更大的量子加速，为在实际输入上实现量子优势提供了路径。

Abstract: Smoothed analysis is a central framework in classical algorithms for explaining the performance of algorithms beyond the worst case, often explaining why algorithms perform well in practice. We initiate a systematic study of its quantum counterpart and show the following results. $(1)$ We show that there is a total function whose smoothed quantum query complexity is exponentially smaller than its classical query complexity. $(2)$ We give near-tight characterizations of smoothed randomized and quantum query complexities for symmetric Boolean functions, unifying the worst-case complexity results of [Beals et al, FOCS'98] and average-case complexity results of [Ambainis and de Wolf, STACS'00]. $(3)$ We study string problems such as pattern matching and edit distance and, in various regimes, give polynomial to superpolynomial quantum speedups. Our main technical ingredients include a near-tight quantum algorithm for $\varepsilon$-approximating the number of collisions between two non-repetitive strings, improving the result of Le Gall and Ng [QIC'22]. Together, our results show that smoothing can reveal larger quantum speedups than worst-case analysis suggests, opening a path towards quantum advantage on more realistic inputs.

</details>


### [99] [QC-Stark: A Multi-Task Benchmark Revealing Capability Dissociations in LLMs Evaluated on Quantum Computing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35581)
*Pranav Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出QC-Stark，一个覆盖11项量子计算任务的大语言模型评测基准，发现整体排名会掩盖任务级别的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测多依赖整体排名，可能掩盖模型在不同具体任务上的表现差异，尤其在量子计算这类多子领域任务中缺乏系统化、可自动验证的评测基准。

Method: 构建QC-Stark基准，涵盖电路构建、调试、编译、纠错和模拟等11项量子计算任务；进行2,750次评测（10个模型 × 11项任务 × 5个难度等级 × 5个随机种子）；使用2参数IRT模型验证测量质量，并进行提示敏感性分析；所有任务通过执行自动验证。

Result: 整体排名与任务级排名在11项任务中有4项的Spearman相关性统计不显著；IRT模型验证了测量质量，提示敏感性分析确认排名在不同提示条件下具有稳健性；所有任务可自动验证，无需人工评估。

Conclusion: LLM在量子计算任务上的整体排名可能误导，需关注任务级评测；QC-Stark提供了可自动验证、公开可用的基准，代码和数据已在Huggingface发布。

Abstract: We introduce QC-Stark, a benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on 11 quantum computing (QC) tasks, spanning circuit construction, debugging, compilation, error correction, and simulation. Across 2,750 evaluations (10 models $\times$ 11 tasks x 5 difficulty levels x 5 seeds), we find that overall rankings mask substantial per-task variation. The Spearman correlation between overall and per-task rankings is statistically insignificant for 4 out of the 11 tasks included in this benchmark. A 2-parameter Item Response Theory (IRT) model validates measurement quality, and prompt sensitivity analysis confirms ranking robustness across prompt conditions. All tasks are auto-verifiable via execution, thus not requiring any manual evaluation. We make the code and data publicly available on Huggingface.

</details>


### [100] [Exponential lower bounds for low-degree strategies in position-based quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35592)
*Isabel M. Moreno-Cuadrado,David Perez-Garcia,Carlos Palazuelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）中实现对角±1酉算符的资源下界，证明在首轮策略对ε的多项式依赖度受限时，存在U_ε需要指数级资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC能否对某些酉算符U_AB实现指数级（关于系统维度n的exp(n^Ω(1))）下界，是量子密码学中的核心开放问题；该下界对基于位置的量子密码安全性、通用量子模拟器最优性及全息量子引力性质均有重要意义，而现有最优下界仅为亚线性。

Method: 考虑由布尔超立方体ε∈{±1}^{n^2}索引的对角±1酉算符族U_ε，其中U_ε|i⟩_A|j⟩_B=ε_{ij}|i⟩_A|j⟩_B。假设所有ε对应的U_ε均能以常数精度在NLQC中实现，且关联策略首轮对ε的依赖为次数O(n^{1/4-δ})的多项式。证明基于特定Banach空间的几何性质（type常数）及取值于其上的布尔函数随机估计。

Result: 若上述条件成立，则至少存在一个U_ε，其在NLQC中的实现所需资源规模为exp(Ω(n^{2δ}/(log n)^2))，即指数级下界。

Conclusion: 在首轮策略对ε的多项式依赖度受限的前提下，对角±1酉算符族中存在NLQC实现需指数级资源的实例，为NLQC指数下界问题提供了新的条件性结果。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) consists on the implementation of a bipartite unitary $U_{AB}$ by two cooperating distant players by means of a two-round protocol with a single intermediate round of simultaneous communication. It is a major open question to know whether there exists a unitary $U_{AB}$ whose implementation in NLQC requires extra quantum systems with dimensions ${\rm exp}(n^{Ω(1)})$, where $n$ is the dimension of systems $A$ and $B$. This type of exponential lower bound is essential for security of position-based quantum cryptography, since the capabilities of the adversaries are precisely described by NLQC. It has also been connected to many other problems, such as the optimality of universal quantum simulators, or the expected properties of holographic quantum gravity. Currently the best lower bounds are only sublinear in $n$.
  In this paper we consider the family of diagonal ${\pm 1}$ valued unitaries $U_{\varepsilon}$, indexed by an element of the Boolean hypercube $\varepsilon \in \{\pm 1\}^{n^2}$, i.e. $U_{\varepsilon}|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B =\varepsilon_{ij} |i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B$. We show that if $U_{\varepsilon}$ can be implemented in NLQC with constant accuracy for all $\varepsilon$, and the dependency on $\varepsilon$ in the first round of the associated strategy is a polynomial of degree $O(n^{\frac{1}{4}-δ})$, then the implementation of at least one $U_{\varepsilon}$ in NLQC requires resources scaling as $\exp\left(Ω\left(\frac{n^{2δ}}{(\log n)^2}\right)\right)$. The proof is based on geometric properties of particular Banach spaces, namely their type constants, together with random estimates of Boolean functions valued on them.

</details>


### [101] [Low-Weight Canonical Logical Bases from Pair-Partition Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35601)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为量子比特CSS码构造完整的规范逻辑基：通过给二元循环置换矩阵对划分(CPM-PP)校验赋予四元系数，构造并归一化逻辑代表元，再展开为二元矩阵。当两个校验系统在不交列集上有可逆子矩阵时，余因子代表元由单个配对多项式的逆规范配对，所生成的配对张成整个逻辑空间；当配对多项式为系数为1的循环移位单项式时，归一化保持代表元的二元权重。文中给出一般块维数与CPM尺寸下的构造、一个示例，并报告了七个二元码的参数、校验秩与基权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子比特CSS码建立完整的规范逻辑基，解决逻辑代表元的系统性构造与规范配对问题，并保持其二元权重性质。

Method: 将四元系数赋给二元循环置换矩阵对划分(CPM-PP)校验；构造并归一化逻辑代表元；展开为二元矩阵；在两校验系统于不交列集上存在可逆子矩阵时，用单个配对多项式的逆对余因子代表元做规范配对；配对多项式为系数1的循环移位单项式时，归一化保持二元权重；给出一般块维数与CPM尺寸下的构造并举例。

Result: 报告七个二元码的参数、校验秩与基权重。代表例参数为[[320,80,14]]、[[448,112,18]]、[[2048,512,24]]；三者在X侧和Z侧的最大校验权重均为10，其规范逻辑代表元在X侧和Z侧的二元权重分别为25、27、27。

Conclusion: 所提出的构造可为量子比特CSS码给出完整的规范逻辑基，配对多项式保证了代表元对张成整个逻辑空间，且在特定单项式条件下归一化保持二元权重，实例验证了该构造在参数、校验秩与基权重方面的有效性。

Abstract: We construct complete canonical logical bases for qubit CSS codes by assigning quaternary coefficients to binary circulant permutation matrix pair-partition (CPM-PP) checks, constructing and normalizing logical representatives, and expanding the result into binary matrices. When the two check systems have invertible submatrices on disjoint column sets, cofactor representatives are canonically paired by the inverse of a single pairing polynomial. The resulting pairs span the entire logical space. When the pairing polynomial is a cyclic-shift monomial with coefficient one, normalization preserves the binary weights of the representatives. We state the construction for general block dimensions and CPM size, work through a corresponding example, and report the parameters, check ranks, and basis weights of seven binary codes. Representative examples have parameters $[[320,80,14]]$, $[[448,112,18]]$, and $[[2048,512,24]]$. All three have maximum check weight 10 on both the X and Z sides. Their canonical logical representatives have binary weights 25, 27, and 27, respectively, on both the X and Z sides.

</details>


### [102] [Succinct Arguments for QMA from Collapsing Hash Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35633)
*James Bartusek,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：仅假设 collapsing hash functions 存在，即可构造 QMA 的 succinct arguments；这是首个仅依赖非结构化 Minicrypt 假设（不足以保证公钥加密）的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 QMA 的简洁论证（succinct arguments）通常依赖更强假设（如公钥加密等）。作者希望将其基础降低到非结构化的 Minicrypt 假设，即仅需 collapsing hash functions，从而减弱密码学假设强度。关键技术障碍在于需要高效、可扩展地生成大量 quantum correlations。

Method: 核心技术贡献是一个 quantum-succinct claw-state generation 协议：仅使用经典通信，将少量 quantum correlations 自举（bootstrap）为任意大量的 claw-state correlations。该协议相较 [Zhang, STOC 2021] 具有更优的轮复杂度、标准模型下的证明，且整体更简洁。由此得到基于 one-way functions 的 quantum-succinct blind delegation of quantum computation 协议，并将其接入 [Bartusek, Liu, and Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026] 的 communication-compression compiler，从而获得 QMA 的 succinct arguments。

Result: 在仅假设 collapsing hash functions 存在的前提下，成功构造了 QMA 的 succinct arguments，为首次仅基于非结构化 Minicrypt 假设实现该目标。同时给出了改进的 claw-state generation 协议及基于 one-way functions 的 quantum-succinct blind delegation 协议。

Conclusion: QMA 的简洁论证可建立在更弱的 Minicrypt 假设之上（collapsing hash functions），突破了此前对更强假设的依赖；其关键技术——量子简洁的 claw-state 生成——在轮复杂度、标准模型可证性与简洁性上均优于先前工作。

Abstract: We prove the existence of succinct arguments for QMA, assuming only the existence of collapsing hash functions. This is the first scheme that relies only on unstructured ``Minicrypt'' assumptions, which are not known to imply public-key encryption.
  Our main technical contribution is a quantum-succinct \emph{claw-state generation} protocol that allows us to bootstrap a small number of quantum correlations into an arbitrarily large number of claw-state correlations, using classical communication only. This improves upon the work of [Zhang, STOC 2021], having better round complexity, a proof in the standard model, and being overall much simpler. This yields a quantum-succinct blind delegation of quantum computation protocol from one-way functions, which we plug into the communication-compression compiler of [Bartusek, Liu, and Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026] to obtain succinct arguments for QMA.

</details>


### [103] [Learning sparse quantum states from single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35648)
*Su-un Lee,Liang Jiang,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究稀疏量子态的学习问题，证明仅用单量子比特测量即可高效学习具有长程纠缠的稀疏态，无需使用纠缠门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏量子态虽有紧凑的经典描述，但可携带长程纠缠，导致无法仅从局部约化密度矩阵重建；此前方法需大量纠缠门来提取信息，这对当前量子设备不友好。

Method: 提出仅使用单量子比特测量的学习算法，针对不同稀疏度情形进行分析：稀疏度 s 为常数、s 随 n 多项式增长，以及稀疏基为已知固定单量子比特基乘积（如泡利算符本征基）的特殊情形。

Result: 当 s 为常数时，算法以多项式样本复杂度和多项式经典计算复杂度学习稀疏态；当 s 随 n 多项式增长时，样本复杂度仍为多项式，但一般不保证高效经典计算；在稀疏基为已知固定单量子比特基乘积时，经典计算复杂度为拟多项式。

Conclusion: 结果建立了无需纠缠门即可高效学习具有长程纠缠的稀疏态，单量子比特测量要求使算法与当前量子设备兼容。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a sparse quantum state, an $n$-qubit quantum state whose density matrix has at most $s$ nonzero matrix entries in an unknown product basis. While such states admit compact classical descriptions, they can carry long-range entanglement that prevents reconstruction from local reduced density matrices alone. Therefore, previous learning approaches addressed such long-range-entangled states using many entangling gates to extract the necessary information. In this work, we show that sparse states can nevertheless be efficiently learned using only single-qubit measurements. Specifically, when the sparsity $s$ is constant, our algorithm can learn sparse states from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity and classical computational complexity. When $s$ grows polynomially with $n$, sparse states can still be learned from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity, although efficient classical computation is not guaranteed in general. In this regime, however, the classical computational complexity becomes quasipolynomial when the state is sparse in an unknown basis that is a product of a known fixed finite set of single-qubit bases (e.g., eigenbases of Pauli operators). These results establish efficient learning of sparse states with long-range entanglement without entangling gates, and the single-qubit measurement requirements make our algorithms compatible with current quantum devices.

</details>


### [104] [Optimal performance in a chaotic Floquet quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35655)
*Lakshmija N K,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究由 N 个均匀相互作用量子比特构成的量子电池：通过在横向施加周期性 kick、并在相互作用方向引入无序进行充电，无序使系统偏离置换对称子空间，从而提升储能；大无序极限下储能可由随机矩阵理论精确计算，且能量涨落趋于可忽略，储能最大；在退相位噪声的存储阶段，大无序下 ergotropy 接近储能，提取效率趋近于 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的优化运行需要同时实现高储能与低能量涨落，而均匀相互作用的置换对称结构通常限制了可存储能量的进一步提升。作者希望借助无序打破置换对称性，探索提升储能、抑制涨落并保持高能量可提取性的充电与存储方案。

Method: 以 N 个均匀相互作用量子比特构成量子电池，从基态出发，在横向施加周期性 kick 并在相互作用方向引入无序来完成充电；分析充电过程中无序导致的置换对称子空间外演化；在大无序极限下利用随机矩阵理论精确计算储能；并以含退相位噪声的存储阶段完成整个电池循环，用 ergotropy 衡量能量可提取性。

Result: 无序使充电动力学离开置换对称子空间，相比无无序情形可存储更多能量；在大无序极限下出现混沌充电相，储能可由随机矩阵理论精确给出；能量涨落随无序增大而减小，并在大无序极限下变得可忽略，而此时储能恰好最大；在含退相位噪声的存储阶段，大无序强度下 ergotropy 接近储能，表明能量几乎可完全提取，效率趋近于 1。

Conclusion: 在均匀相互作用量子比特电池中，相互作用方向的无序与横向周期性 kick 的协同作用可同时实现最大储能与可忽略的能量涨落，并借助随机矩阵理论给出大无序极限下的精确描述；配合退相位噪声下的存储分析，电池可在高无序条件下以接近 unity 的效率提取能量，满足最优运行条件。

Abstract: We study a quantum battery consisting of $N$ qubits interacting uniformly with each other. Starting from its ground state, the battery is charged by introducing periodic kicks in the transverse direction along with adding disorder in the interaction direction. Although the battery Hamiltonian along with the initial state preserves the permutation symmetry, the dynamics in presence of disorder during the charging phase takes the system out of the permutation symmetric subspace, resulting to a larger amount of stored energy as compared to that without disorder. In the limit of large disorder, this interplay brings in a possibility of a chaotic charging phase when stored energy can be exactly calculated using random matrix theory. More importantly, we also find that the energy fluctuations decrease and become negligible in the limit of large disorder, which is also when the energy stored is maximum, a condition highly desirable for the optimum functioning of the battery. We complete the battery cycle by considering the storage phase in the presence of dephasing noise. We find that for larger disorder strengths, the ergotropy remains close to the stored energy, indicating nearly complete energy extractability with an efficiency approaching unity.

</details>


### [105] [The power of oracle access: Optimal sample and query complexity of the abelian state hidden subgroup problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35656)
*Yuhan Liu,Jose Carrasco,Jens Eisert,Armando Bellante*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限阿贝尔群上隐藏子群问题（StateHSP）在样本模型和查询模型中的量子复杂度：查询模型中利用相干访问达到 O(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 并证明匹配下界，样本模型中 Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε) 为充要样本数，从而说明 ε 的二次加速源自量子相干访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在寻找具有超多项式加速的量子算法时，输入访问模型对问题复杂度起关键作用。对于 StateHSP（识别未知量子态对称性的 HSP 量子推广），有限阿贝尔群上已有傅里叶采样算法使用 O(log(|G|)/ε) 份态，但该标度是否为最优一直未定。

Method: 在两种模型下研究阿贝尔 StateHSP：①先前研究的样本模型；②新查询模型——更强的操作自然推广，允许访问态制备酉及其逆。查询模型中给出使用 O(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 次正向与逆向查询的高效量子算法，并证明匹配的 Ω(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 下界（即便在更强的共轭查询和受控查询设置下也成立）；样本模型中证明 Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε) 份态既充分又在信息论上必要（即使允许任意集体测量）。

Result: 查询模型：时间高效量子算法达到 O(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 正向与逆向查询，且 Ω(log(|G/H|)/√ε) 下界在共轭查询与受控查询下仍成立。样本模型：Θ(log(|G/H|)/ε) 份态为充要，即使允许任意集体测量也如此。应用：获得学习稳定子群、定位无纠缠以及识别隐藏平移对称性的更快算法。

Conclusion: ε 上二次改进（1/ε → 1/√ε）真正源于对制备电路的相干访问，而非样本模型所能提供。该结果刻画了阿贝尔 StateHSP 的复杂度，并带来多个下游算法的加速。

Abstract: In the quest to identify further quantum algorithms exhibiting superpolynomial speed-ups, a recurring theme is that the complexity of a problem is largely shaped by the input access model. Here, we study this phenomenon for the state hidden subgroup problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalization of the hidden subgroup problem in which the goal is to identify the symmetries of an unknown quantum state. For finite abelian groups, existing Fourier-sampling algorithms use $O(\log(|G|)/ε)$ copies of the state, but whether this scaling is optimal has remained open. We settle the complexity of the abelian StateHSP in both the previously studied sample model and a new query model, which is a stronger and operationally natural generalization that provides access to the state-preparation unitary and its inverse. In the query model, we give a time-efficient quantum algorithm using $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ forward and inverse queries, and prove a matching $Ω(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ lower bound which holds even in the stronger conjugate-query and controlled-query settings. By contrast, we show that in the sample model, $Θ(\log(|G/H|)/ε)$ copies are both sufficient and information-theoretically necessary, even if one allows for arbitrary collective measurements. Thus, the quadratic improvement in $ε$ genuinely arises from coherent access to the preparation circuit. As applications, we obtain faster algorithms for learning stabilizer groups, locating unentanglement, and identifying hidden translation symmetries.

</details>


### [106] [An Optimal Quantum Linear Systems Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35660)
*Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aram W. Harrow,Robin Kothari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了量子线性系统问题（QLSP）的查询复杂度为 Θ(κ√d·log(1/ε))，并解决了Berry与Childs提出的开放问题，证明任意 N×N 酉矩阵可用 O(√N) 次矩阵元查询以有界误差实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLSP是量子算法领域的核心问题，此前已有大量工作，但上界（O(κd·log(1/ε)) 与 κ√d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}）与下界（Ω(κ·log(1/ε)) 与 Ω(κ√d)）之间存在显著差距。作者希望缩小并最终闭合这一复杂度差距，同时解决Berry与Childs提出的酉矩阵实现查询复杂度的开放问题。

Method: 采用量子查询复杂度的上下界分析技术：一方面构造改进的量子算法，将上界提升至 κ√d·log(1/ε)；另一方面给出匹配的量子下界证明，从而确立紧确复杂度。针对酉矩阵实现问题，利用矩阵元查询模型设计 O(√N) 查询量的实现方案。

Result: 证明QLSP的复杂度为 Θ(κ√d·log(1/ε))，同时改进了此前最优上界与下界；并证明任意 N×N 酉矩阵均可在有界误差下用 O(√N) 次矩阵元查询实现，解决了Berry与Childs的开放问题。

Conclusion: QLSP的查询复杂度自此被完全确定，消除了长期存在的上界与下界差距；此外，酉矩阵的 O(√N) 查询实现结果给出了该任务在矩阵元查询模型下的紧确复杂度刻画。

Abstract: In the quantum linear systems problem (QLSP), we are given query access to a $d$-sparse $N\times N$ matrix $A$ with condition number $κ$, and the ability to prepare a quantum state proportional to a vector $\vec b$. The goal is to output an $ε$-approximation to the quantum state proportional to the solution $\vec x$ of $A\vec{x}=\vec{b}$. Following a long line of work, the best previously known quantum algorithms for the QLSP had query complexities $O(κd\log(1/ε))$ and $κ\sqrt d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}$, while the best known lower bounds were $Ω(κ\log(1/ε))$ and $Ω(κ\sqrt d)$. We improve these bounds and show that the complexity of the QLSP is $Θ(κ\sqrt d\log(1/ε))$. We also resolve an open problem of Berry and Childs by showing that any $N\times N$ unitary can be implemented with bounded error using $O(\sqrt N)$ queries to its matrix entries.

</details>


### [107] [Optimal Query Complexity for Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35668)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在已知能量阈值（位于谱隙中）条件下，将基态制备到迹距离误差 ε 的最优查询复杂度，并给出达到该复杂度的两个算法及匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定基态制备的查询复杂度最优界。在能量阈值已知（比基态能量高至少 Δ/2、比每个激发态能量低至少 Δ/2）的设定下，刻画制备基态的查询复杂度，以明确该问题的最优资源消耗。

Method: 设 U_H 为哈密顿量的 α-block-encoding，唯一基态为 |ψ_0⟩，状态制备 oracle U_I 满足 |⟨ψ_0|U_I|0⟩| ≥ γ。利用常量精度的谱过滤器构造 purifier，并在振幅放大中顺序复合以制备与基态有常量交叠的态：期望查询算法重复"制备+一次高精度谱过滤"直至成功；最坏情况算法使用精度递增的过滤器并限制总查询次数。

Result: 得到两个算法：其一对 U_H 的期望调用次数为 O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+log(1/ε)))，其二最坏情况调用次数为 O((α/(γΔ))log(1/ε))；对 U_I 的调用次数分别为期望 O(1/γ)、最坏情况 O(γ^{-1}log(1/ε))。证明了与期望查询次数匹配的下界，最坏情况下界由 Somma 与 de Wolf [SdW26] 给出。在 (N+1) 维系统上，当 U_H 的期望或最坏情况调用次数为 o((α/Δ)√N) 时，对应的 U_I 界也是最优的。

Conclusion: 在给定谱隙内能量阈值的设定下，基态制备的查询复杂度被精确刻画：两个算法分别达到期望与最坏情况下的最优界，且下界与上界匹配，表明所得复杂度是该问题的最优结果。

Abstract: We determine the optimal query complexity of ground-state preparation to trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ when an energy threshold in the spectral gap is known. Let $U_H$ be an $α$-block-encoding of a Hamiltonian with unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$, and suppose $|\langleψ_0|U_I|0\rangle|\geγ$ for a state-preparation oracle $U_I$. The threshold lies at least $Δ/2$ above the ground-state energy and at least $Δ/2$ below every excited-state energy. We give two algorithms that prepare a state within trace distance $\varepsilon$ of the ground state. One uses $O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ calls to $U_H$ in expectation; the other uses $O((α/(γΔ))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ calls to $U_H$ in the worst case. We prove a lower bound matching the expected query count; the corresponding worst-case lower bound follows from Somma and de Wolf [SdW26]. The respective bounds on calls to $U_I$ are $O(1/γ)$ in expectation and $O(γ^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ in the worst case. On $(N+1)$-dimensional systems, these $U_I$ bounds are also optimal when the expected or worst-case count of $U_H$ calls, respectively, is $o((α/Δ)\sqrt N)$. Both algorithms use a constant-accuracy spectral filter to construct a purifier, which we then sequentially compose during amplitude amplification to prepare a state with constant overlap with the ground state. The expected-query algorithm repeats the preparation followed by one high-accuracy spectral filter until success. The worst-case algorithm uses filters of increasing accuracy and limits the total number of queries.

</details>

### [108] [Discrete quality-factor control in a side-coupled photonic crystal microcavity: evanescent Bloch tunnelling and the finite-cell correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31952)
*Hasan Oguz*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 硅棒二维光子晶体中，点缺陷微腔侧耦W1波导，其共振随缺陷棒半径平方线性移动，品质因数由腔-波导间距（晶格行数）决定，每增加一行约乘7.10，该因子可由最慢倏逝Bloch通道衰减无参数预测；规模化至1550 nm给出634 nm/RIU灵敏度，水的吸收使参考结构Q降至约9200。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究水溶液环境（水性分析物）中光子晶体微腔-波导耦合结构，以评估其作为折射率传感器的性能，并厘清决定共振位置与品质因数（Q）的物理机制及数值仿真可信度所需的条件。

Method: 采用平面波展开法（PWE）与有限差分时域法（FDTD）计算：二维硅棒光子晶体中，点缺陷微腔侧耦至W1波导；分析共振随缺陷棒半径的变化、Q随腔-波导间距（晶格行数）的变化，并用最慢倏逝Bloch通道衰减解释每行因子；采用Fano拟合独立归一化透射谱验证线宽，并计入水在1550 nm的吸收。

Result: 共振位置随缺陷棒半径平方连续移动，符合一阶微扰理论对恢复至晶格位点的介电面积的预测；Q由腔与波导之间相隔的晶格行数决定，在参考缺陷半径下每增加一行乘以7.10，而缺陷棒位移达十分之一晶格周期仅使Q变化不到7%。每行因子在两个缺陷半径下均可由导模波矢处晶体最慢倏逝Bloch通道的衰减无调节参数地给出。规模化至1550 nm时灵敏度为634 nm/RIU，微扰理论可重现；Fano拟合确认线宽。水的吸收使参考几何的Q降低三分之一，上限约为9200。

Conclusion: 在有限计算胞中可信地获取上述数值需两项检验：包层厚度收敛和消除波导端面反射；否则每行因子偏低、Q误差可达两倍。水的吸收显著限制Q提升（约9200），使继续增加行数的收益受限。

Abstract: A point-defect microcavity side-coupled to a W1 waveguide in a two-dimensional photonic crystal of silicon rods immersed in an aqueous analyte is studied by plane-wave expansion and finite-difference time-domain computation. The resonance moves continuously through the transverse-magnetic band gap with the square of the radius of the defect rod, as first-order perturbation theory predicts for the dielectric area restored to the lattice site. The quality factor is set instead by the separation between cavity and waveguide, counted in lattice rows: each added row multiplies it by 7.10 at the reference defect radius, whereas moving the defect rod by up to a tenth of a lattice period changes it by less than 7 per cent. The per-row factor follows, at two defect radii and with no adjustable parameter, from the decay of the slowest evanescent Bloch channel of the crystal at the wavevector of the guided mode. Two checks are needed before such values can be trusted in a finite cell: convergence in cladding thickness and removal of the reflections from the waveguide ends. Without them the per-row factor comes out too low and the quality factor wrong by up to a factor of two. Scaled to 1550 nm the design gives a sensitivity of 634 nm per refractive index unit, which perturbation theory reproduces, and a Fano fit to an independently normalised transmission spectrum confirms the linewidth. The absorption of water at this wavelength lowers the quality factor of the reference geometry by a third and caps it near 9200, which limits the benefit of adding further rows.

</details>


### [109] [Geometry-Controlled Polarization Photocurrents in Scalable PtSe2 Infrared Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32136)
*Eunice Y. Paik,Owen A. Vail,Madaline R. Marland,William A. Beck,Antonio Llopis-Jepsen,Wendy L. Sarney,Jeffery H. Leach,Stefan Heiserer,Nikolas Dominik,Cormac Ó Coileáin,Paul B. Seifert,Georg S. Duesberg,Blair C. Connelly,George J. de Coster*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用可规模化生长的宽面积PtSe₂薄膜，在室温下实现近红外至中波红外的偏振光响应，并发现器件几何结构会重塑偏振响应：有限元电流仿真重现了偏振灵敏响应的空间再分布，将横向偏振响应局域在像元中心附近，从而借助波长相关激光扫描区分对称性允许的光电流与近接触区的二色性光热贡献。近红外响应符合线性二色性光热电电流，中波红外则表现为斜入射下的螺旋度依赖光热贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强度型探测器无法提供偏振等多维光学信息。贵金属硫族化合物PtSe₂因强自旋轨道耦合、材料稳定性、可定制的宽带偏振响应以及与后端制程硅集成兼容等特性，成为可调偏振探测的有前景材料。然而，如何在大面积可扩展薄膜上理解并调控其偏振响应机制（尤其是几何结构对响应空间分布的影响）尚不清晰，需要区分本征光电与光热等不同物理起源。

Method: 采用可规模化、直接生长的宽面积PtSe₂薄膜制备室温近红外至中波红外偏振光电探测器；通过改变器件几何结构考察偏振响应的重塑；使用有限元电流流动仿真重现偏振灵敏响应的空间再分布并定位横向偏振响应；结合波长相关激光光斑扫描，将对称性允许的光电流与近接触区、由二色性介导的光热贡献区分开。

Result: 实现了室温近红外至中波红外的偏振光响应；测量到的偏振响应受器件几何结构重塑；有限元仿真再现了偏振灵敏响应的空间再分布，并将横向偏振响应定位于像元中心附近；近红外波长的空间响应与线性二色性光热电流一致；中波红外测量揭示斜入射下存在螺旋度依赖的光热贡献。

Conclusion: 像元边界工程既是可规模化PtSe₂偏振敏感红外像元的设计杠杆，也是区分不同偏振响应物理机制的诊断工具，为宽带、可集成的偏振红外探测提供路径。

Abstract: Polarization-sensitive photodetectors provide multi-dimensional optical information beyond the capabilities of traditional intensity-based detectors. The noble metal dichalcogenide PtSe2 presents unique opportunities for tunable polarization detection due to its strong spin-orbit coupling, material stability, customizable broadband polarization responses, and direct compatibility with a wide range of substrates for back-end-of-line silicon integration. In this work, we demonstrate room-temperature near-infrared to mid-wavelength infrared polarization photoresponses using scalable, as-grown, wide-area PtSe2 films, and we show that the measured polarization response is reshaped by device geometry. Finite-element current-flow simulations reproduce the observed spatial redistribution of the polarization-sensitive response and localize the transverse polarization response near the pixel center. This geometry-enabled separation allows wavelength-dependent laser spot scans to distinguish symmetry-allowed photocurrents from contact-proximate, dichroism-mediated photothermal contributions. At near-infrared wavelengths, we observe spatial response that is consistent with linear-dichroic photothermoelectric currents, whereas mid-wavelength infrared measurements reveal a helicity-dependent photothermal contribution under oblique illumination. These results identify pixel boundary engineering as both a design lever and a diagnostic tool for scalable PtSe2 polarization-sensitive infrared pixels.

</details>


### [110] [Asymmetric Negative Refraction in Nonlocal Double-Wire Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32501)
*Tiago A. Morgado,Sylvain Lannebère,Enrico M. Renzi,Andrea Alù,Mário G. Silveirinha*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 异质双线超材料通过两个非相连金属线阵列实现损耗非对称双曲色散与强非对称负折射，该效应依赖非局域响应与角度选择性激发，且保持互易性，可覆盖微波至红外频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局域介质中实现剪切型耗散非对称通常需要降低空间对称性；作者希望探索能否借助非局域性在更高空间对称性的结构中模拟此类耗散非对称，并实现方向性/角度选择性的波调控与能量输运。

Method: 构建由两种不同且互不连接的线阵列组成的异质双线超材料；分析其非局域响应与微结构对双曲传播通道的影响，研究自由空间入射波以相反角度选择性激发两条损耗不等的双曲通道，并考察空气-超材料界面上的负折射、透射/吸收的角度非对称性及互易性；讨论从微波到红外频段的金属线阵列可扩展实现。

Result: 该结构支持两个独立的双曲传播通道且损耗不等，可由相反角度入射的自由空间传播波选择性激发；在界面处产生非对称负折射，表现为透射与吸收的强角度非对称，同时保持互易性；该现象可由金属线阵列在微波至红外宽频段实现。

Conclusion: 非局域性可在无需降低空间对称性的情况下模拟剪切型耗散非对称，异质双线超材料为非互易式（仍互易）的方向性能量传输与角度选择波控提供新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that heterogeneous double-wire metamaterials, comprising two dissimilar and nonconnected wire arrays, enable loss-asymmetric hyperbolic dispersion and strongly asymmetric negative refraction. We unveil that, owing to their strongly nonlocal response and distinctive microstructure, such geometry supports two independent hyperbolic propagation channels with unequal losses, which can be selectively excited by free-space propagating waves incident at opposite angles. This angular selectivity gives rise to asymmetric negative refraction at the air-metamaterial interfaces, characterized by strong angular asymmetry in transmission and absorption, while preserving reciprocity. This phenomenon is scalable and can be realized with metallic wire arrays over a broad frequency spectrum extending from microwave to infrared frequencies, showing that nonlocality can emulate shear-like dissipative asymmetry that, in local media, requires lower spatial symmetry. Our findings open new avenues for directional energy transport and angle-selective wave control in photonic platforms.

</details>


### [111] [Inverse-Designed Time-Varying Multilayers for Programmable Frequency-Momentum Light Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32541)
*Mohammad M. Asgari,Viktar Asadchy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出多层空时周期结构的逆设计框架，联合优化空间介电分布与时间调制，实现频率-动量多通道调控与频率-动量梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态介质难以实现频率转换、谐波产生及频率与动量联合调控；空时变化电磁结构可解锁此类波控机制，但缺乏对空间介电分布与时间调制剖面进行联合逆设计的通用框架。

Method: 构建由多层像素化层组成的平台，层沿一个或两个横向方向周期排列并在时间上谐波调制；将空间介电分布与时间调制剖面联合优化，直接合成复杂的多通道频率-动量散射响应。算例同时考虑连续与二值介电分布，并公开优化框架的数值实现。

Result: 演示两类功能：对三个透射空时通道的幅度与相位进行独立调控；生成频率-动量梳。连续与二值介电分布两种情形均得到验证。

Conclusion: 逆设计的时间变化多层结构为可编程频率-动量复用及空时波包合成提供了紧凑且通用的途径；优化代码开放发布以促进可复现性与后续发展。

Abstract: Space-time-varying electromagnetic structures unlock wave-control mechanisms inaccessible to static media, including frequency conversion, harmonic generation, and joint control over frequency and momentum. Here, we introduce an inverse-design framework for multilayer space-time-periodic structures in which the spatial permittivity distribution and temporal modulation profile are optimized jointly. The proposed platform comprises multiple pixelated layers that are periodic along one or both transverse directions and harmonically modulated in time, enabling the direct synthesis of complex multichannel frequency-momentum scattering responses. We demonstrate two representative functionalities: independent control over the amplitudes and phases of three transmitted spatiotemporal channels and the generation of a frequency-momentum comb. Across these examples, we consider both continuous and binary permittivity distributions. To facilitate reproducibility and further development, we make the numerical implementation of the optimization framework freely available. These results demonstrate that inverse-designed time-varying multilayers provide a compact and versatile route toward programmable frequency-momentum multiplexing and the synthesis of space-time wave packets. The optimization code is made openly available to facilitate reproducibility and further development of the proposed inverse-design framework.

</details>


### [112] [Reconfigurable Linear Optical Transformations in a Single Integrated Multimode Waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32683)
*Manoah van der Worp,Jonas Krimmer,Ivo M. Vellekoop,Pepijn W. H. Pinkse,Stefan Rothe*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于单一多模波导+分布式热光微扰的全集成波前整形方案，借助可微物理模型与梯度优化，实现20模衍射极限聚焦（功率保持>99%）及最高32×32的可编程线性光变换（相关性>99%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波前整形依赖由体光学元件组成的自由空间系统，体积大、难集成；而紧凑、全集成的波前整形方案研究相对匮乏，因此需要探索可在芯片上实现波前调控与可编程线性光变换的新范式。

Method: 1) 概念上采用单根多模波导，通过分布式热光微扰调控多模传播；2) 建立可微物理模型，将器件响应写成可反向传播的映射；3) 使用基于梯度的优化求解波前整形与目标变换；4) 用空间模式数N、可调元件数、微扰强度与模式耦合范围刻画设计权衡；5) 引入辅助模式（ancillary modes）以抑制耦合外溢。

Result: 1) 在20个空间模式下实现衍射极限聚焦，且保持超过99%的输入光功率，理论上聚焦极限可维持；2) 扩展到完整输入—输出变换，实现最高32×32的Sylvester–Hadamard矩阵操作，相关性超过99%；3) 减少可调元件数时需更强单点微扰，导致更强更宽的模式耦合并超出目标模式；4) 引入辅助模式后，仅用5N²（N=4）个可调元件即实现4×4变换，相关性达93.8%±4.8%，最好实现为98.3%。

Conclusion: 热光调控多模波导为全集成波前整形与可编程线性光变换提供了一条可行路径，在经典与量子光子处理中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Wavefront shaping enables control over optical fields for applications ranging from imaging to photonic information processing. While conventional wavefront shaping relies on free-space systems comprising bulk optical components, compact and fully integrated approaches are comparatively unexplored. Here, we introduce a concept based on a single multimode waveguide with distributed thermo-optic perturbations for controlling multimode propagation. We develop a differentiable physical model and numerically demonstrate shaping the outgoing wavefront and programmable linear optical transformations, using gradient-based optimization. We achieve diffraction-limited focusing with 20 spatial modes and show that the theoretical focusing limit can be retained while maintaining more than $99\%$ of the input optical power. We extend this approach to complete input--output transformations and implement Sylvester--Hadamard matrix operations with dimensions up to $32\times32$ with correlations exceeding $99\%$. Reducing the number of tunable elements requires stronger individual perturbations, resulting in stronger and broader mode coupling that extends beyond the target modes. To mitigate this effect, we consider ancillary modes and realize a $4\times4$ transformation that reaches $93.8\%\pm4.8\%$ correlation with only $5N^2$ tunable elements, where $N=4$ denotes the number of spatial modes. The best-performing realization reaches $98.3\%$ correlation with the target. Our results demonstrate that thermo-optic control of multimode waveguides offers an approach to fully-integrated wavefront shaping and programmable linear optical transformations, with potential applications in classical and quantum photonic processing

</details>


### [113] [Vision-Language Agents for Active Perception in Optics Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32918)
*Ryan Lopez,Sachin Vaidya,Seou Choi,Serena Landers,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究通用视觉语言模型（VLM）能否在无密集标量目标条件下，基于视觉反馈实现闭环科学实验控制；以光学系统为测试平台，结果表明VLM可估计执行器响应、通过干预消解观测歧义并在信号稀疏时主动获取信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM正被 increasingly 用于科学工作流，但其作为智能体直接依据视觉反馈操控实验的能力仍缺乏探索。许多实验任务并不天然提供密集的预设数值目标，有用信号可能稀疏、间歇或视觉上模糊；因此更通用的实验智能体应能解读视觉观测、采取行动获取有效反馈，并依据行动后果调整行为。

Method: 在光学实验平台评测通用VLM的闭环控制能力。设置三个实验系统以分离不同能力：迈克尔逊干涉仪、双镜光学腔、四镜光学中继。智能体观察相机图像，直接发送执行器与测量指令，并保留交互历史；控制过程中不接收人工设计的标量目标。实验同时与匹配的仿真对照。

Result: 在上述实验与匹配仿真中，给定任务特定的自然语言指导后，VLM能够估计执行器-响应关系、通过干预消解模糊观测，并在信号稀疏时主动生成有信息量的视觉反馈。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预训练多模态模型可作为实验闭环中的重要决策智能体；研究同时确立了光学作为科学智能体视觉推理与主动感知的物理落地测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being used in scientific workflows, but their ability as agents to directly control laboratory experiments from visual feedback remains underexplored. This capability is important because many laboratory tasks do not naturally provide dense, pre-defined numerical objectives: informative signals can be sparse, intermittent, or visually ambiguous. A more general laboratory agent should instead be able to interpret visual observations, take actions to acquire useful feedback, and adapt its behavior based on the consequences of those actions. We study whether general-purpose VLMs can perform this kind of closed-loop scientific control using experimental optics as a testbed. We evaluate agents on three experimental systems that isolate distinct capabilities: a Michelson interferometer, a two-mirror cavity, and a four-mirror optical relay. The agents observe camera images, directly issue actuator and measurement commands, and retain their interaction history without receiving an engineered scalar objective during control. Across these experiments and matched simulations, we find that, given task-specific natural-language guidance, VLMs can estimate actuator-response relationships, resolve ambiguous observations through intervention, and actively create informative visual feedback when signals are sparse. These results suggest that pretrained multimodal models can serve as important decision-making agents within the experimental loop. Our work also establishes optics as a physically grounded testbed for visual reasoning and active perception in scientific agents.

</details>


### [114] [Optical Möbius Snails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33071)
*Qiang Wang,Pedro A. Quinto-Su,Xi Xie,Xiangsheng Xie,Chenghou Tu,Yijie Shen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用skyrmionic beam紧聚焦在三维空间生成莫比乌斯带连续族，形成"光学莫比乌斯蜗牛"偏振拓扑织构，并实现基于Skyrme数的可切换调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑态研究是多个物理系统的活跃前沿，莫比乌斯带作为经典拓扑结构近年来启发了诸多光学物理研究；本文旨在将莫比乌斯概念拓展到高维光学体系。

Method: 以携带Skyrme数的skyrmionic beam作为入射光场，通过紧聚焦生成三维空间中的连续莫比乌斯带族；开展全视场偏振层析（full-field polarization tomography）测量，并调节入射光束的Skyrme数实现织构切换控制。

Result: 在紧聚焦下获得了蜗牛状偏振拓扑织构，实现了全视场偏振层析表征，并证明了通过改变入射skyrmionic beam的Skyrme数可对该光学蜗牛织构进行可切换调控。

Conclusion: 研究在低维与高维莫比乌斯几何之间建立了直接桥梁，为探索复杂空间拓扑与定制化光-物质相互作用提供了新平台。

Abstract: The exploration of topological states has emerged as a vibrant frontier across diverse physical systems. Among these, the Möbius strip stands out as a canonical structure that has recently inspired intriguing optical physics.In this work, we extend this concept into the high-dimensional realm by using an optical analogue-the optical Möbius snail. Specifically, employing a skyrmionic beam as the input field, tight focusing yields a continuous family of Möbius strips that exists in three-dimensional space, giving rise to a snail-like polarization topological texture. Furthermore, we demonstrate full-field polarization tomography and achieve switchable control of this optical snail texture by tuning the Skyrme number of the incident skyrmionic beam. Our findings establish a direct bridge between low- and high-dimensional Möbius geometries, opening a new platform for exploring complex spatial topology and tailored light-matter interactions.

</details>


### [115] [Tailored multipolar optical trapping with higher-order structured light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33294)
*Nabendu Mishra,Nick Vamivakas,Eileen Otte*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究结合矢量聚焦与广义Lorenz-Mie理论，系统计算了高阶柱矢量/标量光束对Mie尺度硅纳米球的三维净辐射力与陷阱稳定性，预测了高效gray trapping及可配置的尺寸依赖gray/dark trapping。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偶极光力研究多局限于梯度力计算与聚焦标量光束，对Mie尺度粒子高阶多极响应的gray trapping及复杂结构光场中的捕获行为认识不足，需要更完备的理论框架探索定制化捕获景观。

Method: 结合矢量聚焦与广义Lorenz-Mie理论，研究高阶柱矢量与标量光束的紧聚焦特性（横向强度梯度、三维电场分量），计算水中Mie尺度硅纳米球的三维净辐射力，并通过力雅可比评估局部捕获稳定性。

Result: 在八极Mie共振附近预测出矢量光束的高效稳定gray trapping；发现可实现尺寸依赖、可配置的gray/dark trapping场几何，并获得亚波长粒子间距。

Conclusion: 偏振、照明结构与粒子参数可作为半径依赖多极捕获的互补调控手段，为亚波长粒子分选、捕获辅助纳米图案化及光结合研究提供理论基础。

Abstract: Dipolar optical forces can confine Rayleigh-sized dielectric particles towards or away from intensity maxima, commonly known as bright and dark trapping. Structured light illumination opens up opportunities for customized trapping landscapes, unveiling the possibility of gray trapping, especially for larger Mie particles that can excite a higher-order multipolar response. While previous studies have primarily focused on calculations of the gradient force for some exemplary, focused scalar beams, we not only extend these analyses by combining vectorial focusing with generalized Lorenz-Mie theory, but also consider more complex trapping fields. Motivated by their unique tight focusing behavior, shaping strong transverse intensity gradients and three-dimensional electric field components, we explore higher-order cylindrical scalar and vector beams. We theoretically investigate multipolar trapping of Mie-regime silicon nanospheres in water, calculating the three-dimensional net radiation force and assess local trapping stability through the force Jacobian. Near an octupolar Mie resonance, we predict stable gray trapping with enhanced efficiency for vector beams. Further, we find field geometries enabling configurable, particle-size-dependent gray/dark trapping with subwavelength inter-particle spacing. These results establish polarization, illumination structure and particle parameters as complementary controls for radius-dependent multipolar trapping, providing a basis for potential applications in sub-wavelength particle sorting, trapping-assisted nanopatterning and optical binding investigations.

</details>


### [116] [Propagation of pulse fluctuations of multimode sources in optical fibers and waveguides with arbitrary chromatic dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33320)
*José Capmany*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将Marcuse对随机相位多模光源脉冲涨落的分析推广至任意色散、啁啾、有限模式线宽、模式分配噪声及有限接收带宽情形；发现长光纤上涨落仅取决于群延迟曲线形状：单调延迟使各模式分离，对称延迟则保留成对模式产生持续拍噪声，色散将重叠模式拍频下变频至基带而无法被接收机滤除，检测功率服从随机相量统计，高斯近似的衰落概率误差可达数个量级，模式分配噪声随色散增大并在带宽受限链路中占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marcuse原有的随机相位多模光源脉冲涨落分析适用条件受限，未涵盖任意色散、啁啾、有限模式线宽、模式分配噪声与有限接收带宽等实际链路因素，需建立更一般的理论以准确刻画长光纤传输中的脉冲涨落与衰落特性。

Method: 在Marcuse分析框架基础上进行理论推广，引入任意色散、啁啾、有限模式线宽、模式分配噪声和有限接收带宽；通过分析群延迟曲线形状对模式间拍频的作用，并采用随机相量统计描述检测功率，与高斯近似结果进行对比。

Result: 长光纤上脉冲涨落仅取决于群延迟曲线形状：单调延迟使模式彼此分离，而对称延迟保留具有相同强度分布的成对模式，导致持续拍噪声；色散将重叠模式的拍频下变频至基带，接收机无法滤除。检测功率遵循随机相量统计，高斯估计的衰落概率可偏差数个量级；模式分配噪声随色散增大，在带宽受限链路中占主导地位。

Conclusion: 该推广分析表明，多模光源脉冲涨落与衰落概率的准确评估必须考虑群延迟曲线形状、色散引起的基带拍噪声及模式分配噪声，传统高斯近似在预测衰落概率时可能严重失准，尤其在带宽受限链路中。

Abstract: We extend Marcuse analysis of pulse fluctuations of randomly phased multimode sources to arbitrary chromatic dispersion, chirp, finite mode linewidth, mode partition noise and finite receiver bandwidth. On long fibers the fluctuations depend only on the shape of the group delay curve, a monotonic delay separates the modes, whereas a symmetric delay leaves pairs of modes with identical intensity profiles and a persistent beat noise. Dispersion downconverts the beats of overlapping modes to baseband, where receivers cannot filter them. The detected power follows random phasor statistics, and Gaussian estimates of fade probability can be wrong by orders of magnitude. Mode partition noise grows with dispersion and dominates in band limited links.

</details>


### [117] [Supergaussian pulse distortion in single-mode fibers with arbitrary dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33404)
*Jose Capmany*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将Marcuse单模光纤脉冲畸变理论从高斯脉冲推广到任意阶超高斯脉冲，涵盖线性/边缘集中啁啾、单线或多线光源及任意色散系数，用Wigner分布矩精确求解rms输出宽度，并分析超高斯脉冲的色散敏感性与脉冲序列干涉特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marcuse理论虽为任意谱宽光源的高斯脉冲提供了闭式结果，但缺乏对超高斯脉冲及相关复杂条件下的解析描述，需要扩展以覆盖更一般的脉冲形态与光源、啁啾、色散情形。

Method: 将理论扩展至任意阶超高斯脉冲，支持线性啁啾或边缘集中啁啾、单线及多线光源、任意数目色散系数；通过Wigner分布的矩精确计算rms输出宽度，涉及的脉冲矩化为Gamma函数的有限和。

Result: 超高斯脉冲对高阶色散比高斯脉冲明显更敏感，啁啾压缩效率更低；在零色散波长附近其rms宽度由弱谱旁瓣主导，会高估脉冲能量的展宽。脉冲序列中相邻脉冲不重叠时干涉不改变各时间矩，但局部重分布能量，可见度由光源谱经脉冲谱滤波决定。

Conclusion: 扩展后的理论为超高斯脉冲在单模光纤中的畸变提供了闭式解析工具，揭示了其与高斯脉冲在色散敏感性、啁啾压缩及零色散区行为上的差异。

Abstract: Marcuse theory of pulse distortion in singlemode fibers provides closed-form results for Gaussian pulses launched from sources of arbitrary spectral width. We extend the theory to supergaussian pulses of arbitrary order with linear or edge-concentrated chirp, single or multiline sources and an arbitrary number of dispersion coefficients. The rms output width is obtained exactly from the moments of the Wigner distribution, and all the pulse moments involved reduce to finite sums of Gamma functions. Supergaussian pulses are considerably more sensitive than Gaussian pulses to higher order dispersion and are compressed less efficiently by chirp. Near the zero-dispersion wavelength their rms width is governed by weak spectral side lobes and overestimates the broadening of the pulse energy. For pulse sequences, interference between neighboring pulses leaves all time moments unchanged when the input pulses do not overlap, but it redistributes energy locally with a visibility set by the source spectrum filtered by the pulse spectrum.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Aperture PPG with MAPIS: Spatial Optical and Temporal Coherence Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33790)
*Shuguang Wang,Yuanjing Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究基于单台MAPIS设备采集44段30秒记录，分析多孔径PPG中DC强度、搏动性AC、灌注指数(PI)与时间相干性在红/红外通道间的空间关系，发现AC随DC亚线性增长、低DC孔径PI更高，且低DC/低AC孔径反而具有更高的自相关与心脏周期性谱集中度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反射式PPG通常将组织光学响应压缩到一个或少数探测器通道，掩盖了孔径相关的光学信息。作者希望利用MAPIS同时采样多孔径信号的能力，刻画DC强度、搏动性AC、灌注指数与波形时间相干性之间的空间关系，从而揭示被单通道PPG平均掉的规律。

Method: 使用单台MAPIS设备采集44段、每段30秒的红光与红外记录，对每个孔径通道提取DC强度、搏动性AC、灌注指数(PI)和波形时间相干性（自相关、心脏周期性谱集中度），并分析这些指标随DC强度的空间变化及跨波长差异。

Result: AC随DC在两个波长上均呈亚线性增长，导致低DC孔径处PI更高，符合一阶相对路径灵敏度解释。时间相干性呈现反向空间趋势：低DC、低AC孔径的自相关更高、心脏周期性谱集中度更强。红外信号的时间相干性一致高于对应红光信号。

Conclusion: 多孔径PPG能够解析出光学强度、分数搏动灵敏度与波形相干性之间可重复的空间关系，而这些关系在传统单通道PPG中大多被平均掉。本研究确立了设备内部的关系，并为后续关于光子路径机制的受控研究提供动机。

Abstract: Conventional reflectance photoplethysmography (PPG) typically reduces tissue optical responses to one or a few detector channels. Matrix Pinhole Image Sensing (MAPIS) instead simultaneously samples multiple aperture-dependent optical signals. We analyzed 44 recordings of 30 seconds each from a single MAPIS device to characterize the spatial relationships among DC intensity, pulsatile AC, perfusion index (PI), and temporal coherence across Red and infrared channels. AC increased sublinearly with DC at both wavelengths, resulting in higher PI toward lower-DC apertures, consistent with a first-order relative path-sensitivity interpretation. A second counterintuitive spatial trend was observed in temporal coherence: lower-DC and lower-AC apertures exhibited higher autocorrelation and greater cardiac-periodic spectral concentration. IR signals also showed consistently higher temporal coherence than corresponding Red signals. These observations demonstrate that multi-aperture PPG resolves reproducible spatial relationships among optical intensity, fractional pulsatile sensitivity, and waveform coherence that are largely averaged together in conventional single-channel PPG. The present study establishes within-device relationships and motivates controlled studies of the underlying photon-path mechanisms.

</details>


### [119] [Tapered Fibre Fabry-Pérot Cavities for the Generation of Near-Visible, Phase-locked, and Broadband Kerr Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34037)
*Matthew Macnaughtan,Yiqing Xu,Miro Erkintalo,Stéphane Coen,Stuart G. Murdoch*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用锥形光纤法布里-珀罗谐振腔实现近可见光波段宽带克尔频率梳，突破二氧化硅在短波长的强正常色散限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短波长处几乎所有材料（包括二氧化硅）都存在强正常色散，难以满足克尔频率梳所需的净反常色散条件，从而阻碍近可见光/可见光波段相干频率梳的产生。

Method: 将微米直径锥形二氧化硅光纤引入脉冲驱动法布里-珀罗腔，通过几何色散工程在近可见光区获得净反常色散；锥形光纤的强模式约束使克尔非线性增强一个数量级以上，显著降低非线性结构形成的阈值驱动功率；结合精确的腔制备控制，实验并数值验证多种非线性结构。

Result: 实验与数值结果一致地展示了丰富的相干锁相非线性结构：常规反常色散腔孤子、光谱带宽超过16 THz（750–800 nm）的零色散孤子、开关波频率梳以及宽带拉曼孤子；其中拉曼孤子在近可见光波段属首次报道。

Conclusion: 锥形光纤法布里-珀罗谐振腔是一种通用平台，可用于近可见光乃至可见光波段的相干频率梳产生。

Abstract: We demonstrate the generation of near-visible, broadband Kerr frequency combs using a tapered fibre Fabry-Pérot resonator. By incorporating micron-diameter tapered silica fibre into a pulsed-driven Fabry-Pérot configuration, we exploit geometric dispersion engineering to overcome the strong normal dispersion intrinsic to silica (and nearly all materials) at short wavelengths, achieving net anomalous dispersion in the near-visible regime. The strong mode confinement of the tapered fibre simultaneously enhances the Kerr nonlinearity by over an order of magnitude relative to standard fibre, substantially reducing the threshold drive power for nonlinear structure formation. Through precise control of the cavity fabrication, we experimentally demonstrate and numerically confirm a rich suite of coherent, phase-locked nonlinear structures, including conventional anomalous dispersion cavity solitons, zero dispersion solitons with spectral bandwidths exceeding 16 THz between 750 nm and 800 nm, as well as switching wave combs and broadband Raman solitons, the latter also reported here for the first time in the near-visible regime. Our results establish the tapered fibre Fabry-Pérot resonator as a versatile platform for near-visible and potentially visible coherent frequency comb generation.

</details>


### [120] [Multimode oscillation and its route to chaos in transverse-mode-coupled optomechanical resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34040)
*Motoki Asano,Lalit Maharjan,Hiroshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种横向模式耦合的光力平台，利用工程化集体机械模式与光学可调非线性相互作用，实现稳定的双模与三模自持振荡，并观测到倍频分数化及确定性混沌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性振子的集体现象（同步、分岔、混沌）十分丰富，但实验中获取多模非线性动力学存在困难：低阶内共振易导致模式锁定，从而抑制稳定的多模振荡。因此需要一种能规避该限制、可扩展且可调的平台来研究高维非线性动力学。

Method: 构建横向模式耦合的光力平台，将工程化的集体机械模式与光控非线性相互作用相结合。所设计的横向机械模式可抑制低阶内共振，支持稳定的双模和三模自持振荡。通过调节激光频率来调控非线性动力学行为。

Result: 改变激光频率时，依次观测到拍频分数化（Δf/2 与 Δf/3），随后出现具有增强轨迹发散特征的宽频状态，与确定性混沌相符。

Conclusion: 横向模式耦合的光力谐振器可作为可扩展、可调的平台，用于探索相互作用机械网络中的高维非线性动力学。

Abstract: Nonlinear oscillators exhibit rich collective phenomena including synchronization, bifurcation, and chaos. Experimentally accessing multimode nonlinear dynamics, however, remains challenging because low-order internal resonances readily induce mode locking and suppress stable multimode oscillation. Here, we demonstrate a transverse-mode-coupled optomechanical platform that combines engineered collective mechanical modes with optically controllable nonlinear interactions. The engineered transverse mechanical modes enable stable two- and three-mode self-sustained oscillations while suppressing trivial low-order internal resonances. By varying the laser frequency, we observe successive beat-frequency fractionalization (Δf/2 and Δf/3), followed by a broadband state exhibiting enhanced trajectory divergence consistent with deterministic chaos. These results establish transverse-mode-coupled optomechanical resonators as a scalable and tunable platform for exploring high-dimensional nonlinear dynamics in interacting mechanical networks.

</details>


### [121] [Tensor-Engineered Van der Waals NbOCl2 Resonant Metasurface for Polarization-entangled Bell State Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34095)
*Xin Zeng,Wenna Du,Yun-Kun Wu,Yuyang Zhang,Zhiyong Zhang,Kwok Kwan Tang,Ruihuan Duan,Xiaotian Bao,Yiyang Gong,Yuexing Xia,Yutong Zhang,Jinhan Zhao,Yubo Tian,Yangguang Zhong,Chuanxiu Jiang,Yubin Wang,Jianhui Fu,Shuai Yue,Guankui Long,Qing Zhang,Zheng Liu,Xi-Feng Ren,Xinfeng Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于NbOCl2的共振范德华非线性超表面，利用本征光学各向异性在正交偏振通道间重分配二阶非线性极化率张量，平衡沿b、c轴的自发参量下转换路径，实现保真度达92%的偏振纠缠光子对产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振纠缠光子对是量子信息技术的核心资源，但兼具紧凑性与内禀可控纠缠的源仍难以实现。范德华非线性材料（如NbOCl2）可提供原子级厚度的量子光源平台，然而其作为天然材料固有的晶体各向异性限制了对量子态的调控自由度。

Method: 构建基于NbOCl2的共振范德华非线性超表面，利用材料本征光学各向异性设计偏振依赖的非线性响应；借助各向异性光学色散选择性调控共振模式，并进一步剪裁共振模式，在正交偏振通道之间重新分配有效二阶非线性极化率张量，以平衡b轴与c轴的自发参量下转换路径。

Result: 各向异性色散带来的共振模式选择性调控使c轴方向非线性响应增强三个数量级；通过共振模式剪裁实现正交偏振通道间二阶非线性极化率张量的再分配，从而平衡两条自发参量下转换路径，测得偏振纠缠光子产生的保真度最高达92%。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了"超表面赋能的非线性光学工程"作为增强与调控范德华材料中量子光产生的有效策略，为可扩展量子光子学平台提供了实现路径。

Abstract: Polarization-entangled photon pairs are essential resources for quantum information technologies, yet realizing compact sources with intrinsically controllable entanglement remains challenging. Van der Waals (vdW) nonlinear materials such as NbOCl2 provide atomically thin platforms for quantum light generation, yet their native crystalline anisotropies as natural materials impose limitations on accessible quantum states. Here, we develop a resonant vdW nonlinear metasurface based on NbOCl2 that exploits its intrinsic optical anisotropy to engineer polarization-dependent nonlinear responses. The anisotropic optical dispersion enables selective manipulation of resonant modes, resulting in a three-order-of-magnitude enhancement of the nonlinear response along the c axis. By further tailoring these resonant modes, we redistribute the effective second-order nonlinear susceptibility tensor between orthogonal polarization channels, balancing the spontaneous parametric down-conversion pathways along the b and c axes. This enables polarization-entangled photon generation with a measured fidelity of up to 92%. Our work establishes metasurface-enabled nonlinear optical engineering as a strategy for enhancing and controlling quantum light generation in vdW materials, providing a pathway toward scalable quantum photonic platforms.

</details>


### [122] [Stimulated Electro-optic Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34105)
*Violet Workman,Gaurav Bahl*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文揭示了压电性为受激布里渊散射(SBS)提供的额外增益通道，提出"受激电光散射"新机制，并在铌酸锂波导中验证了增益增强、抑制及常规理论预判为零处的显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中SBS通常仅归因于光弹效应与介电边界运动，忽略了压电介质中伴随机械运动的电场作用。作者旨在探究压电性是否提供了此前未被识别的SBS新路径，以扩展现代SBS理论。

Method: 将线性电光"Pockels"散射与其互易的χ⁽²⁾介导光驱动效应（二者共同耦合光场与压电体中伴随机械运动的电场）形式化纳入现代SBS理论，并将其应用于单片铌酸锂波导进行验证。

Result: 发现布里渊增益的大幅增强与抑制，并在常规理论预判布里渊相互作用为零的情形下意外出现大增益；首次识别出纯电光SBS过程，即"受激电光散射"。

Conclusion: 电光张量被确立为压电介质中布里渊相互作用的关键调控参数，为独立于光弹性和折射率对比度来工程化光子-声子耦合提供了新自由度。

Abstract: Stimulated Brillouin Scattering (SBS) couples light to acoustic waves and underpins applications ranging from sensing and signal processing to quantum photonics. In solids, this interaction is generally attributed to photoelasticity and the motion of dielectric boundaries. Here we show that piezoelectricity opens an additional, previously unrecognized pathway that can substantially reshape Brillouin gain. This contribution arises from the combined action of linear electro-optic ``Pockels'' scattering and its reciprocal $χ^{(2)}$-mediated optical drive, which couple optical fields to the electric fields that accompany mechanical motion in piezoelectrics. We formally incorporate these effects into the modern theory of SBS and apply it to monolithic lithium niobate waveguides. In addition to large enhancement and suppression of the Brillouin gain, we surprisingly find occurrences of large gain where conventional theory would otherwise have predicted zero Brillouin interaction. These instances are the first identification of purely electro-optic SBS, a process we term stimulated electro-optic scattering, as it only involves interactions between electromagnetic fields even though a mechanical excitation is present. Our results establish the electro-optic tensor as a key governing parameter for Brillouin interactions in piezoelectric media and provide a new degree of freedom for engineering photon-phonon coupling independent of photoelasticity and index contrast.

</details>


### [123] [Monolithically integrated photonic neural network at driven-dissipative criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34171)
*Yuming Zhang,Ruoran Wang,Jingcheng Li,Hailong Zhou,Jianji Dong,Zhipei Sun,Xinliang Zhang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一种单片集成的有源光子神经网络（APNN），利用驱动-耗散临界性作为物理计算资源，在硅光芯片上实现表征增强、时序信息保持与鲁棒计算，并在分类、ECG特征提取与联想记忆任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对自适应表征、时序处理与不确定性下鲁棒运算的需求日益增长，但传统光子神经架构依赖预定义运算与分立功能模块，难以利用物理系统的内禀动力学实现智能。

Method: 构建单片集成有源光子神经网络：通过平衡光学激发与耗散使系统工作于临界区；集成光电非线性阵列与可重构对称光耦合矩阵于硅光芯片，形成紧凑闭环以实现循环物理计算；利用驱动-耗散动力学完成表征增强、长程时序特征提取与基于吸引子弛豫的模式恢复。

Result: 仅用100个训练样本和44个可训练参数实现95.2%非线性分类准确率；在-30 dB极低信噪比下仍保持鲁棒；同一动力学支持ECG信号的长程时序特征提取与受损模式的联想恢复。

Conclusion: 驱动-耗散临界性可作为统一机制实现表征增强、时序信息保持与容错计算，为智能从内禀物理动力学中涌现的可扩展光子系统提供路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly demands computing architectures capable of adaptive representation, temporal information processing, and robust operation under uncertainty. However, conventional photonic neural architectures typically rely on predefined computational operations and separate functional modules, limiting the ability of physical systems to exploit their intrinsic dynamics for intelligence. Here, we demonstrate a monolithically integrated active photonic neural network (APNN) that harnesses driven-dissipative criticality as a physical computational resource. By balancing optical excitation and dissipation, the system operates near a critical regime where weak input-dependent variations are amplified into separable representations while stable attractor dynamics are preserved. The APNN integrates an optoelectronic nonlinear array and a reconfigurable symmetric optical coupling matrix on a silicon photonic chip, enabling recurrent physical computation within a compact closed loop. We demonstrate nonlinear classification with 95.2% accuracy using only 100 training samples and 44 trainable parameters. And it remains robust at an ultralow signal-to-noise ratio of -30 dB. Beyond static recognition, the same driven-dissipative dynamics enable long-range temporal feature extraction from electrocardiogram signals and associative recovery of corrupted patterns through attractor-based relaxation. These results establish driven-dissipative criticality as a unified mechanism for representation enhancement, temporal information retention, and error-resilient computation, providing a pathway toward scalable photonic systems in which intelligence emerges from intrinsic physical dynamics.

</details>


### [124] [Surface-Normal Electric Spin in Obliquely Excited Parallel-Slit SPP Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34224)
*Naoki Ichiji*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文揭示斜入射s偏振光照射平行狭缝阵列时，可产生沿表面法向交替分布的自旋织构，其周期随入射角连续可调，且衬底侧与空气侧激发下的自旋分布因SPP与激发场是否干涉而呈现本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性依赖的光-物质相互作用需要控制局域电场旋转，但在斜入射条件下生成可控的表面法向自旋织构十分困难，因此需要一种可行的调控方案。

Method: 采用周期平行狭缝阵列结构，在斜入射s偏振光下激发表面等离极化激元（SPP）；对比衬底侧与空气侧激发两种构型，并用简单干涉模型解释自旋织构的形成。

Result: 斜入射s偏振照明产生空间交替的表面法向电自旋，周期由入射角连续调谐；衬底侧激发时自旋分布遵循常规自旋流关系，而空气侧激发时SPP与激发场干涉导致不同空间周期，并使自旋极值可与场强极大值重合。简单干涉模型可复现这两种截然不同的自旋织构。

Conclusion: 通过入射角与激发侧的选择，可在狭缝阵列上实现表面法向自旋织构的连续调谐与定性切换，为斜入射下局域电场旋转的手性控制提供了简单有效的途径。

Abstract: Handedness-dependent light-matter interactions require control of local electric-field rotation, but generating controlled surface-normal spin textures is difficult under oblique illumination. Here, we show that oblique s-polarized illumination of a periodic array of parallel slits produces spatially alternating surface-normal electric spin, with its period continuously tunable by the incidence angle. We further show that the spin texture changes qualitatively between substrate- and air-side excitation depending on whether the SPP field interferes with the excitation field. Under substrate-side excitation, the spin distribution follows the conventional spin-flow relation, whereas under air-side excitation, interference between the SPP and excitation fields produces a distinct spatial periodicity and allows the spin extrema to coincide with the field-intensity maxima. A simple interference model reproduces these contrasting spin textures.

</details>


### [125] [Contact lens with stretchable distributed-feedback laser for intraocular pressure monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34476)
*S. A. Ivanov,I. M. Fradkin,E. S. Musikhina,R. V. Kirtaev,A. A. Khrebtov,A. A. Vyshnevyy,A. A. Marchenko,V. R. Solovei,I. P. Radko,A. V. Arsenin,V. S. Volkov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将基于表面调制超薄F8BT层的分布反馈（DFB）激光器集成到PDMS软接触镜中，利用Γ点对称保护连续域束缚态产生窄线宽激光，其波长随光栅应变直接偏移，从而突破基于规则结构周期的光学应变传感器的空间不确定度原理限制，在仿体眼模型上实现0.027 nm/mmHg灵敏度与1.2 mmHg标定残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
