Motivation: 青光眼诊疗需要连续监测眼内压（IOP），但临床依赖间歇性诊室测量，无法捕捉关键的昼夜波动。智能接触镜是有吸引力的连续监测平台，光学应变传感器因高灵敏度和天然读出方式而备受关注。然而，现有光学传感器多通过规则结构（光栅或光子晶体）的周期来推断应变，其精度受不确定度原理的根本限制，在接触镜内可用的毫米级长度尺度上性能严重受限。

Method: 在软PDMS接触镜中集成基于表面调制超薄F8BT染料层的分布反馈（DFB）激光器，工作于Γ点的对称保护连续域束缚态，产生窄激光线，其波长随光栅应变直接偏移（该量不受空间不确定度原理约束）。DFB结构通过紫外全息光刻制备，并经简单的float-off工艺转移到镜片上，形成可拉伸、透明、聚合物兼容且本征可扩展的传感器；在定制人工眼模型上测试，其压力诱导变形尺度与报道的人眼相当。

Result: 在仿体条件下，传感器实现0.027 nm/mmHg的灵敏度和1.2 mmHg的标定残差，达到与眼压计监测相关的水平；目前主要受辅助读出设备的限制，仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 通过在接触镜中集成DFB激光器并利用对称保护连续域束缚态，将应变读出从受不确定度原理限制的周期量转变为不受该约束的波长偏移量，为连续IOP监测提供了一种可拉伸、透明、聚合物兼容且可扩展的光学传感方案，仿体性能已达眼压监测相关水平，后续提升主要取决于读出设备。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of intraocular pressure (IOP) is essential for the diagnosis and management of glaucoma, yet existing clinical methods rely on intermittent in-clinic measurements that miss critical diurnal fluctuations. Smart contact lenses are an attractive platform for continuous IOP tracking, and optical strain sensors are particularly promising thanks to their high sensitivity and natural readout. Most such optical sensors infer strain from the period of a regular structure - a grating or photonic crystal - whose deformation is read out optically. However, the precision of all sensors based on the period of regular structures is fundamentally bounded by the uncertainty principle, which severely limits performance at the millimeter length scales available inside a contact lens. Here, we address this limitation by integrating a distributed-feedback (DFB) laser, based on a surface-modulated ultrathin F8BT dye layer, into a soft polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) contact lens. Operating at a symmetry-protected bound state in the continuum at the Γ point, the device produces a narrow lasing line whose wavelength shifts directly with grating strain - a quantity not constrained by the spatial uncertainty principle. The DFB structure is fabricated by UV holographic lithography and transferred onto the lens by a simple float-off process, yielding a stretchable, transparent, polymer-compatible sensor that is intrinsically scalable. Tested on a custom artificial eye model whose pressure-induced deformation is comparable in scale to that reported for the human eye, the sensor achieves a sensitivity of 0.027 nm/mmHg and a calibration residual of 1.2 mmHg under phantom conditions, a level relevant to tonometric monitoring, with substantial headroom currently limited mainly by auxiliary readout equipment.

</details>


### [126] [Surface Acoustic Wave-Mediated Brillouin Temperature Sensor on Thin-Film Lithium Niobate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34739)
*Varun Marathan Kovil,Justus Nölkensmeier,Gladys Jara-Schulz,Pratham Kulkarni,Silia Babel,Christian Golla,Rajkumar Jadhav,Christof Eigner,Laura Padberg,Christine Silberhorn,Birgit Stiller*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次在绝缘体上薄膜铌酸锂（TFLN）芯片上实现基于表面声波（SAW）的受激布里渊散射温度传感器，获得24.8 m⁻¹W⁻¹的布里渊增益和-0.73 MHz/°C的温度系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜铌酸锂为强布里渊相互作用提供了平台，但如何在TFLN中充分利用受激布里渊散射（SBS）实现各类布里渊应用仍待深入探索，尤其是缺乏基于SAW的布里渊温度传感的系统性研究。

Method: 在x切TFLN波导中利用强模间布里渊相互作用，采用SAW介导的传感机制直接感知周围环境温度；结合实验与数值模拟，并针对铌酸锂的各向异性，测量布里渊频率随传播方向（相对晶体y轴0°、10°、20°、30°）的温度依赖关系。

Result: 测得布里渊增益为24.8 ± 1.3 m⁻¹W⁻¹，温度系数为-0.73 ± 0.02 MHz/°C；系统兼容现有集成光子架构且具有较高光功率效率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对TFLN-on-insulator芯片上SAW介导的布里渊温度传感器进行了全面的实验与数值研究，验证了其在集成光子温度传感中的可行性。

Abstract: The emergence of thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) has paved the way for realizing strong Brillouin interactions in lithium niobate platforms, enabling new opportunities in integrated photonics. However, fully harnessing stimulated Brillouin scattering (SBS) in TFLN for the plethora of Brillouin applications remains an exciting area of research, with significant opportunities for further exploration and technological advancement. Here, we present the first comprehensive experimental and numerical study of the surface acoustic wave (SAW)-mediated Brillouin temperature sensor on a TFLN-on-insulator chip. The proposed technique directly senses the surrounding environment via SAWs, providing an optical-power-efficient platform compatible with existing integrated photonic architectures. Exploiting the strong intermodal Brillouin interactions in x-cut TFLN waveguides, we report a high Brillouin gain of 24.8$ \pm $1.3\, m$^{-1}$W$^{-1}$ and a temperature coefficient of $-0.73$ $\pm$ 0.02\,MHz/$^\circ$C. Considering the anisotropic nature of lithium niobate, we investigate the temperature dependence of the Brillouin frequency for the propagation directions 0$^\circ$, 10$^\circ$, 20$^\circ$, 30$^\circ$ relative to the crystallographic y-axis.

</details>


### [127] [A three-phase framework for photon scattering in porous nanocomposites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34870)
*Khalid Alhammadi,Daniel Carne,Xiulin Ruan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验测定六种多孔BaSO4-丙烯酸与TiO2-丙烯酸涂层的散射系数，提出显式考虑气孔散射的三相散射框架，将平均绝对对数误差从0.667降至0.379，揭示气孔是可独立调控的光散射来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔纳米复合材料中的光散射广泛存在于自然现象与光学技术中，但难以理解。现有主流理论通常将此类材料视为由颗粒与基体组成的双相体系，忽略了多孔复合材料中固有的气孔，导致理论与实验存在偏差。

Method: 实验测定六种不同颗粒体积分数的BaSO4-丙烯酸和TiO2-丙烯酸涂层的散射系数；将复合材料表示为颗粒-基体与颗粒-空气两个耦合散射子系统，构建显式计入气孔诱导散射的三相散射框架。

Result: 传统双相模型对BaSO4涂层散射显著低估、对TiO2涂层散射显著高估；三相模型准确捕捉观测趋势，将平均绝对对数误差由0.667降至0.379。

Conclusion: 气孔是光学散射的基本且可独立调控的贡献因素，该框架为工程化高散射多孔纳米复合材料提供了新的物理认识与设计原则。

Abstract: Light scattering in heterogeneous media underpins a broad range of natural phenomena and optical technologies, yet remains difficult to understand in porous nanocomposites. State-of-the-art theories generally treat these materials as two-phase systems consisting of particles and a matrix, overlooking the air pores that are intrinsically present in porous composites. Here we experimentally determine the scattering coefficients of six BaSO4-acrylic and TiO2-acrylic coatings spanning a range of particle volume fractions and develop a three-phase scattering framework that explicitly accounts for pore-induced scattering. By representing the composite as two coupled scattering subsystems, particle-matrix and particle-air, the framework resolves discrepancies between theory and experiment: conventional two phase models substantially underpredict scattering in BaSO4 coatings while overpredicting scattering in TiO2 coatings, whereas the three-phase model accurately captures the observed trends and reduces the mean absolute logarithmic error from 0.667 to 0.379. This framework identifies air pores as a fundamental and independently controllable contributor to optical scattering, providing new physical insights and design principles for engineering highly scattering porous nanocomposites.

</details>


### [128] [Nonlinear Quantum Optics in Epsilon-Nesar-Zero Nanospheres: a Macroscopic Quantum Electrodynamics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35146)
*Sonia Alipour,Marco Ornigotti*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于宏观量子电动力学与格林张量共振态展开的理论框架，用于处理epsilon-near-zero（ENZ）纳米球中的量子非线性过程，并以三次谐波产生为例推导了效率与量子涨落的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENZ纳米球中光与物质的非线性相互作用在量子尺度上尚缺乏统一的理论处理工具，尤其是需要一种能够同时描述生成效率与量子涨落、并适用于任意泵浦量子态的方法。

Method: 采用宏观量子电动力学结合纳米腔中格林张量的共振态（RSs）展开，以三次谐波产生（THG）为案例，将生成效率和生成非线性场的量子涨落表示为入射泵浦场不同量子态的解析函数。

Result: 得到了THG效率及生成非线性场量子涨落随泵浦场量子态变化的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至光子纳米结构中任意量子非线性过程的描述。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework for handling quantum nonlinear processes in epsilon-near-zero (ENZ_ nano spheres, based on macroscopic quantum electrodynamics and the resonant states (RSs) expansion of the Green's tensor in nano cavities. Using third-harmonic generation (THG) as a case study, we derive analytical expressions for the generation efficiency, and the quantum fluctuations of the generated nonlinear field, as a function of different quantum states of the impinging pump field. Finally, we discuss how to generalise this theoretical framework to arbitrarily quantum nonlinear processes in photonic nanostructures.

</details>


### [129] [Generating Vector-Vortex $γ$ Photons by Nonlinear Compton Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35208)
*Yong-Zheng Ren,Mamutjan Ababekri,Jun-Lin Zhou,Feng Wan,Qian Zhao,Zhong-Peng Li,Kun Xue,Ya-Qing Huang,Zhao-Hui Chen,Zhong-Feng Xu,Jian-Xing Li*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出利用椭圆偏振激光脉冲中的非线性康普顿散射生成矢量涡旋γ光子的新方法，通过调控激光椭偏率实现相反绕数OAM模的相干布居，产生自旋-OAM不可分离的光子态；在中等强度线偏振激光下，MeV γ光子模式对的concurrence可达1，实现最大不可分离的矢量涡旋态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量涡旋光子因偏振与轨道角动量（OAM）之间的不可分离耦合，在光操控、量子通信、核光子学等领域具有重要应用价值，但其在γ射线波段的产生仍具挑战性，本文旨在解决这一难题。

Method: 采用椭圆偏振激光脉冲中的非线性康普顿散射机制，通过裁剪/调控激光椭偏率，引导多光子吸收过程相干布居具有相反绕数±ℓ、并与正交圆偏振相绑定的OAM模，从而生成自旋-OAM不可分离的光子态。

Result: 对中等强度线偏振激光（无量纲振幅a₀~1），MeV γ光子的模式对concurrence（自旋-OAM纠缠的0到1度量）达到1，实现最大不可分离的径向型或方位角型矢量涡旋态；激光振幅还可进一步控制可访问的OAM谱。

Conclusion: 该方法为MeV矢量涡旋光子提供了生成途径，为核尺度结构化光子学和高能量子信息开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Vector-vortex photons, characterized by a nonseparable coupling between polarization and orbital angular momentum (OAM), offer opportunities for optical manipulation, quantum communication, nuclear photonics, etc. However, their generation in the $γ$-ray regime remains challenging. Here, we put forward a novel method to generate vector-vortex $γ$ photons via nonlinear Compton scattering in elliptically polarized laser pulses. We reveal that tailoring laser ellipticity directs the multiphoton absorption to coherently populate OAM modes with opposite winding numbers, $\pm \ell$, tied to orthogonal circular polarizations, producing nonseparable spin-OAM photon states. For a linearly polarized laser of moderate intensity (dimensionless amplitude $a_0 \sim 1$), the mode-pair concurrence--a 0-to-1 measure of spin-OAM entanglement--reaches unity for MeV $γ$ photons, realizing maximally nonseparable radial- or azimuthal-type vector-vortex states. The laser amplitude further controls the accessible OAM spectrum. Our method offers a route to MeV vector-vortex photons, opening a new avenue for nuclear-scale structured photonics and high-energy quantum information.

</details>


### [130] [Contactless Continuous-Variable Quantum Optical State Conditioning With Classical MmWave Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35324)
*Niloy Ghosh,Sarang Pendharker*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立海森堡绘景框架，首次实现用数字调制毫米波束对连续变量量子光态的无接触调制，揭示毫米波相位对压缩态维格纳空间演化和正交不确定度的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子光调控依赖片上金属互连和复杂电子控制电路，难以与新兴毫米波/太赫兹通信技术无缝衔接，亟需一种无线、非接触的量子光调控理论基础。

Method: 建立面向毫米波到光子数据转换的海森堡绘景理论框架，分析数字调制经典毫米波束与连续变量量子光态（相干态、压缩态）相互作用，在维格纳空间追踪其演化。

Result: 毫米波相位变化直接影响调制光相干态与压缩态的维格纳空间演化；经典毫米波相位不仅控制位移，还调制压缩态的正交不确定度，可将信息嵌入多个量子光自由度。

Conclusion: 该工作为无需片上金属互连和复杂电子电路的量子光无线调控奠定理论与分析基础，为集成量子光子学与新兴毫米波/太赫兹通信技术的无缝桥接开辟关键路径。

Abstract: This paper establishes the Heisenberg's picture framework for seamless mmWave-to-photonic data transduction. Based on this framework, contactless modulation of continuous-variable (CV) quantum optical states with digitally modulated classical mmWave beams is shown for the first time. Our analysis reveals the direct influence of mmWave phase variation on the Wigner-space evolution of modulated optical coherent and squeezed states. Furthermore, the analysis indicates that classical mmWave phase not only governs the displacement but also modulates the quadrature uncertainty of squeezed states, revealing a novel technique for embedding information onto multiple quantum-optical degrees of freedom. This work builds the theoretical and analytical foundation for wireless conditioning of quantum light without conventional on-chip metallic interconnects and complex electronic control circuitry, thereby opening a key pathway toward seamless bridging of integrated quantum photonics with emerging mmWave or THz communication technology.

</details>


### [131] [Plasmon-Enhanced Second-Harmonic Generation in Atomically Thin Crystalline Silver Nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35477)
*Saad Abdullah,Philipp K. Jenke,Andrew P. Weber,Álvaro Rodríguez Echarri,Vahagn Mkhitaryan,Fadil Iyikanat,Frederik Schiller,Philip Walther,J. Enrique Ortega,Lee A. Rozema,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用原子级厚度外延银膜中的电子量子限域与等离激元增强的协同作用，突破中心对称介质中二阶非线性被禁戒的限制，通过纳米条带/纳米三角形阵列实现可调谐的强二次谐波产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料本征非线性光学响应弱，且中心对称介质的二阶过程受对称性禁戒，在深亚波长、超薄体积中实现高效频率转换极为困难，这制约了非线性纳米光子学的发展。

Method: 外延生长原子级厚度晶体银膜，利用垂直方向电子量子限域产生电子量子阱增强的非线性响应；再将其加工成周期性纳米条带与纳米三角形阵列，支持红外局域表面等离激元共振，通过强近场限域进一步增强二次谐波产生，并借由几何调控实现共振及谐波频率的谱调谐。

Result: 原子级厚度银膜展现出与电子量子阱相关的增强非线性响应；图案化结构相对于未图案化薄膜显著提升了二次谐波产生，且通过精确控制纳米结构几何可实现等离激元共振及增强谐波频率的谱调谐。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了在量子限域金属中激活稳健二阶非线性的途径，使本征尺寸效应与等离激元共振协同作用；高质量外延生长与微芯片制造技术的兼容性，为片上频率转换、传感与量子光子学应用的超紧凑非线性光学器件提供了可扩展路线。

Abstract: The intrinsically weak nonlinear optical response of existing materials, further constrained by symmetry-forbidden second-order processes in centrosymmetric media, severely limits efficient frequency conversion in deeply subwavelength, ultrathin volumes. Addressing this challenge is crucial for the development of nonlinear nanophotonics. Here, we show that atomically thin, epitaxially grown crystalline silver films circumvent these restrictions through the interplay of vertical electronic quantum confinement and lateral plasmonic enhancement. We fabricate atomically thin films that exhibit an enhanced nonlinear response associated with electronic quantum wells, and subsequently pattern them into periodic nanoribbon and nanotriangle arrays sustaining infrared localized surface plasmon resonances. Strong near-field confinement in these structures further boosts second-harmonic generation compared to unpatterned films. Precise control over nanostructure geometry enables spectral tuning of the plasmonic resonance, and consequently, the enhanced harmonic frequency. Our findings establish an approach for activating robust second-order nonlinearities in quantum-confined metals, where intrinsic size effects and plasmonic resonances act synergistically. The compatibility of high-quality epitaxial growth with microchip fabrication technology offers a scalable route toward ultracompact nonlinear optical components for on-chip frequency conversion, sensing, and quantum photonic applications.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Nanodiamond Sensing of Stray Fields during Domain Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31950)
*Kin On Ho,Hamza Abudayyeh,Andrew Perez,Hui-Ping Chang,Liang Juan Chang,Junwei Tong,Can Cui,Jean Anne C. Incorvia,Xiaoqin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用承载NV色心系综的纳米金刚石作为局域量子传感器，定量测量CoFeB条带在磁畴翻转过程中的杂散磁场，并通过微磁学模拟与约束拟合方法解释和提取空间非均匀场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度杂散磁场的定量测量对理解和优化磁存储与逻辑器件至关重要，但现有探针难以同时实现高空间分辨率与低侵入性，因此需要一种简单、可转移且定量的局域磁场表征手段。

Method: 将含NV色心系综的纳米金刚石置于CoFeB条带附近作为局域量子传感器，在磁场驱动畴翻转过程中测量ODMR谱；针对共振部分分辨的情况，提出全约束拟合流程以稳健提取磁场；并用微磁学模拟计算静磁边界场进行对照解释。

Result: ND传感器根据其相对条带位置分辨出三种不同局域响应：边缘处正频率跳变、条带外侧附近意外的负跳变、中心附近几乎无变化；这些对比信号可由微磁学模拟得到的静磁边界场定量解释。

Conclusion: NV-ND磁强计是一种简单、可转移且定量的方法，可用于表征薄膜与自旋电子器件中空间非均匀的磁场分布。

Abstract: Quantitative measurement of nanoscale stray magnetic fields during domain reversal is important for understanding and optimizing magnetic memory and logic devices. Yet, achieving high spatial resolution with minimally invasive probes remains challenging. Here we demonstrate nanodiamonds (NDs) hosting an ensemble nitrogen vacancy (NV) centers as local quantum sensors of domain-reversal stray fields in a CoFeB strip. During magnetic-field-driven reversal, the ND sensors resolve three distinct local responses depending on their positions relative to the strip: a positive frequency jump at the edge, an unexpected negative jump just outside the strip, and a negligible change near the center. These contrasting signals are quantitatively explained by magnetostatic boundary fields from micromagnetic simulations. We further implement a fully constrained fitting procedure for optically detected magnetic resonance spectra, enabling robust field extraction even when resonances are partially resolved. Together, these results establish NV-ND magnetometry as a simple, transferable, and quantitative approach for characterizing spatially heterogeneous magnetic fields in thin-films and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [133] [Magnetic Q-balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32059)
*A. J. Balseyro Sebastian,Keisuke Ohashi,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究准一维手性磁体中带电孤子分支，比较反铁磁（二阶时间导数）与铁磁（Berry相位动力学）两种动力学闭合下，同一静态DM相互作用、易轴各向异性与Zeeman耦合的磁功能量所支持的极角Q-ball与Q-kink解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态手性能量相同的情况下，带电孤子机制如何依赖底层磁动力学尚不清楚；需要厘清DM相互作用、易轴各向异性与Zeeman场在不同动力学闭合下对带电孤子存在条件的影响。

Method: 将静态磁功能量与两种动力学闭合配对：反铁磁模型采用二阶时间导数，铁磁模型采用Berry相位动力学。在静态DM相互作用选定的螺旋分支上，问题约化为关于序参量极角的一维可解析系统；由约化有效势的曲率与非零转折点的存在推导极Q-ball的存在条件；反铁磁情形下进一步分析Q-kink的显式剖面、电荷、能量与约化扇区裂变判据；铁磁情形下分析Berry相位导致的转动频率作为移位Zeeman场的效应。

Result: 反铁磁情形下允许频率窗口对称，并可被DM耦合与Zeeman场的联合效应完全关闭；零Zeeman场时同一约化支持反铁磁Q-kink，并给出显式剖面、电荷、能量及约化扇区裂变判据。铁磁情形下Berry相位使转动频率充当移位Zeeman场，北/南极带电液滴由移位转动的相反符号选择；铁磁力学问题的形式化极到极解一般不对应有限能量磁孤子，因为Berry项不重整化物理Hamiltonian。

Conclusion: 即使在静态手性能量相同的情况下，带电孤子机制仍显著依赖于底层磁动力学；反铁磁与铁磁动力学导致不同的存在条件、频率窗口与解的结构，Berry相位在铁磁情形中仅起移位Zeeman场作用而不改变物理Hamiltonian。

Abstract: We study charged soliton branches in quasi-one-dimensional chiral magnetic systems with Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction, easy-axis anisotropy, and Zeeman coupling. The same static magnetic functional is equipped with two different dynamical completions: an antiferromagnetic model with second-order time derivatives and a ferromagnetic model with Berry-phase dynamics. On the helical branch selected by the static DM interaction, the problem reduces to an analytically tractable one-dimensional system for the polar angle of the order parameter. We derive the existence conditions for polar Q-balls from the curvature of the reduced effective potential and the presence of a nonzero turning point. In the antiferromagnetic case, the allowed frequency window is symmetric and can be completely closed by the combined effect of the DM coupling and the Zeeman field. At zero Zeeman field, the same reduction also supports antiferromagnetic Q-kinks, for which we obtain explicit profiles, charges, energies, and reduced-sector fission criteria. In the ferromagnetic case, the Berry phase makes the rotation frequency act as a shifted Zeeman field. As a result, north- and south-pole charged droplets are selected by opposite signs of the shifted rotation. We also show that a formal pole-to-pole solution of the ferromagnetic mechanical problem does not generally correspond to a finite-energy magnetic soliton, because the Berry term does not renormalize the physical Hamiltonian. These results clarify how charged-soliton mechanisms depend on the underlying magnetic dynamics, even when the static chiral energy is the same.

</details>


### [134] [In-Memory AM Demodulation Using an All-Silicon Independent-Dual-Gate Gain-Cell Memory with $<10^{-22}$ A Leakage Determined by Single-Electron Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32122)
*Katsuhiko Nishiguchi,Toshiaki Hayashi,Kensaku Chida,Takase Shimizu,Gento Yamahata,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种全硅独立双栅存储器，漏电流低于10^-22 A，保持时间超1000 s，可实现存内AM解调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向存内传感与计算的超低泄漏存储器受到广泛关注，但硅基存储器因访问晶体管漏电，难以获得足够长的保持时间，无法胜任模拟信号处理。

Method: 采用全硅独立双栅（independent-dual-gate）存储结构，通过单电子计数统计推断漏电流；利用极低漏电特性，在不扰动存储电荷的前提下利用访问晶体管的亚阈值非线性。

Result: 推断漏电流低于10^-22 A，实测保持时间超过1000 s；基于该特性实现了存内幅度调制（AM）解调。

Conclusion: 该存储器为超低功耗信号处理与存内传感提供了一种CMOS兼容平台。

Abstract: Ultra-low-leakage memories are attracting increasing attention for in-memory sensing and computing. However, achieving sufficiently long retention in silicon memories for analog signal processing remains challenging because of leakage through the access transistor. In this work, we demonstrate an all-silicon independent-dual-gate memory whose leakage current, inferred from single-electron counting statistics, is below $10^{-22}$ A, giving a measured retention time exceeding 1000 s. Owing to the extremely low leakage, the subthreshold nonlinearity of the access transistor can be exploited without disturbing the stored charge, enabling in-memory amplitude-modulation (AM) demodulation. The proposed memory provides a CMOS-compatible platform for ultra-low-power signal processing and in-memory sensing.

</details>


### [135] [Enhancing magnonic frequency combs via geometric nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32173)
*Yushun Fang,Weichao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示磁化矢量单位模约束所导致的几何非线性可作为独立非线性资源生成磁振子频率梳，通过横向Floquet工程将横向驱动转化为纵向参量调制，在低频极限下以低阈值实现梳状谱产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁振子频率梳（MFC）依赖动态非线性，通常要求高功率阈值和严格的动量守恒条件，限制了其性能与应用。作者意在寻找一种不依赖动态非线性的替代机制，以降低阈值并拓展MFC的生成途径。

Method: 基于磁化矢量单位模约束所固有的几何非线性，采用横向Floquet工程，将横向驱动转换为纵向参量调制。在低频极限下，将四粒子过程化简为等效的双磁振子调制，从而在<span style="color:#ff0000">线性</span>区实现梳状谱生成，并分析调制幅度随驱动场的二次标度及几何谐波带来的平坦度增强。

Result: 几何非线性可实现低阈值的MFC生成，且无需满足传统动态非线性对高功率与严格动量守恒的要求；调制幅度随驱动场呈二次方标度，且由几何谐波带来的梳谱平坦度得到增强。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对频率梳与磁振子非线性相互作用的理解，为提升磁振子频率梳性能提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Magnonic frequency combs (MFCs) generated via internal magnetic nonlinearities have exhibited rich physics beyond their optical counterparts. However, existing approaches rely on dynamic nonlinearity, which typically demands high power thresholds and stringent momentum conservation. Here we show that the geometric nonlinearity intrinsic to magnetic systems, originating from the unit-norm constraint of the magnetization vector, can serve as an independent nonlinear resource for MFC generation. Through transverse Floquet engineering, this geometric constraint converts a transverse drive into a longitudinal parametric modulation. In the low-frequency limit, the four-particle process reduces to an effective two-magnon modulation, enabling low-threshold comb generation in the linear regime, with the modulation amplitude scaling quadratically with the driving field and enhanced flatness arising from geometric harmonics. These results provide a deeper understanding of frequency combs and magnon nonlinear interactions, and offer a new theoretical foundation for enhancing MFC performance.

</details>


### [136] [Helicity-Controlled Hall Transport in Hybrid Topological Magnetic Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32372)
*Anton V. Hlushchenko,Julia Kharlan,Mykhailo I. Bratchenko,Aleksei V. Chechkin,Jarosław W. Kłos,Oleg A. Tretiakov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的“自旋-空间”变换框架，系统研究不同螺旋度的混合自旋织构（斯格明子、反斯格明子、双麦韧子及反双麦韧子），并通过解析理论与微磁模拟揭示螺旋度与电流驱动输运之间的关系，可实现霍尔角的连续调控、完全抑制与任意面内方向定向操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁织构可作为自旋流驱动的信息载体，但其运动通常伴随霍尔效应而偏离电流方向，限制了输运效率与可控性。

Method: 建立统一的自旋-空间变换框架，结合解析理论与微磁模拟，研究不同螺旋度的混合自旋织构；推导广义 Thiele 方程的解析解，识别螺旋度作为几何控制参数并给出霍尔角的简洁表达式；用 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert 动力学下的微磁模拟验证。

Result: 将螺旋度确认为几何控制参数，给出霍尔角的简洁表达式，实现霍尔角的连续调谐、完全抑制以及沿任意面内方向的确定性操控；微磁模拟证实所生成的自旋织构在 LLG 动力学下保持稳定，并验证了解析预测。

Conclusion: 螺旋度可作为实现拓扑磁织构可编程输运的通用控制参数。

Abstract: Topological magnetic textures can serve as information carriers driven by spin currents. However, their motion is generally accompanied by a Hall effect that deflects them from the current direction, limiting transport efficiency and controllability. We develop a unified spin-space transformation framework enabling a systematic study of hybrid spin textures with different helicities, such as skyrmions, antiskyrmions, bimerons, and antibimerons. Combining analytical theory with micromagnetic simulations, we establish the relation between helicity and current-driven transport. An analytical solution of the generalized Thiele equation identifies helicity as a geometric control parameter and yields a simple expression for the Hall angle, enabling its continuous tuning, complete suppression, and deterministic steering along arbitrary in-plane directions. Micromagnetic simulations confirm that the generated spin textures remain stable under Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert dynamics and validate the analytical predictions. These results establish helicity as a versatile control parameter for programmable transport of topological magnetic textures.

</details>


### [137] [Electrically switchable one-dimensional quadrupolar excitons in lateral double heterojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32392)
*Ryo Kitaura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在单一半导体单层中，通过两个相邻的横向界面构建WS2-MoS2-WS2双异质结，采用有效质量两粒子哈密顿量研究一维电荷转移激子；零电场下界面间耦合产生奇偶宇称分裂的无永久偶极激子态，外加垂直电场可将低能态从四极子叠加态（二次斯塔克位移）连续转变为单界面偶极激子（近线性位移），实现电可重构的一维四极激子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索利用两个相邻横向界面提供的空间自由度，在单一半导体单层内实现对一维电荷转移激子的电场调控，并建立基于连续介质模型的可重构四极激子方案。

Method: 采用有效质量两粒子哈密顿量并纳入屏蔽库仑相互作用，对WS2-MoS2-WS2双异质结进行空间分辨求解；考虑左右界面等价时零电场情况以及垂直界面方向的电场作用，并开展投影测试以分析最低双重态对交叉区域响应的贡献。

Result: 零电场时界面间耦合产生能量分裂的偶宇称与奇宇称激子态，永久偶极为零。电场使最低态由四极子叠加（二次斯塔克位移）转变为以单界面偶极激子为主（近线性位移）。以1.5-nm宽MoS2条带为例，结合能约为104和100 meV，双重态分裂为4.6 meV，交叉电场为0.87 V/μm；投影测试表明最低双重态主导交叉区附近响应。条带宽度对耦合的调节远强于对结合能的调节。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连续介质模型路径，可用于实现电可重构的一维四极激子，并表明通过横向界面几何与条带宽度可调控低场斯塔克灵敏度。

Abstract: Two neighboring lateral interfaces provide a spatial degree of freedom for controlling one-dimensional charge-transfer excitons within a single semiconductor monolayer. We investigate a type-II WS2-MoS2-WS2 double heterojunction using an effective-mass two-particle Hamiltonian with a screened Coulomb interaction. For equivalent left and right interfaces at zero electric field,inter-interface coupling produces energetically split even- and odd-parity exciton states, each with zero permanent dipole. An electric field perpendicular to the interfaces continuously converts the lower state from a quadrupolar superposition with a quadratic Stark shift into a predominantly single-interface dipolar exciton with an approximately linear shift. The spatially resolved calculation gives binding energies of approximately 104 and 100 meV, a doublet splitting of 4.6 meV, and a crossover field of 0.87 V/um for a representative 1.5-nm MoS2 strip. Projection tests show that the lowest doublet controls the response near this crossover. Strip width tunes the coupling much more strongly than the binding energy, providing geometric control of the low-field Stark sensitivity. These results establish a continuum-model route to electrically reconfigurable one-dimensional quadrupolar excitons.

</details>


### [138] [Finite-temperature phase transitions across spin symmetries in two-dimensional magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32644)
*Chen Chen,Moritz A. Goerzen,Megha Arya,Lionel Calmels,Yongping Du,Dongzhe Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟研究经典二维海森堡模型在易面、各向同性、易轴区的有限温度磁性，揭示特征温度受有限尺寸效应与自旋对称性共同调控，并将易面区的BKT理论偏离归因于涡旋-反涡旋对的增殖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内禀二维磁体的研究进展要求建立跨不同自旋对称类的有限温度相变统一认识，但特征温度如何随磁各向异性、自旋对称性与系统尺寸演化尚不清晰。

Method: 采用大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，连续遍历易面、各向同性与易轴区间，考察经典二维海森堡模型的有限温度磁性；分析BKT物理、磁化强度与热容给出的特征温度，并结合涡旋-反涡旋对增殖进行理论与数值论证。

Result: 特征温度强烈受有限尺寸效应与磁晶各向异性诱导的自旋对称性相互作用影响，各向同性海森堡区最显著，向易面与易轴极限明显降低；易面区由磁化和热容独立估计的特征温度显著偏离BKT理论预言，却与XY模型已报道的异常一致，并与涡旋-反涡旋对增殖相关。

Conclusion: 有限易面海森堡磁体的热力学特征温度（包括对BKT理论的偏离）取决于有限尺寸自旋关联与涡旋增殖的相互作用；结果澄清了有限二维磁体中实验与技术相关温度标度随磁各向异性、自旋对称性和系统尺寸的演化规律。

Abstract: Recent advances in intrinsic two-dimensional (2D) magnets have created a need for a unified understanding of finite-temperature phase transitions across different spin-symmetry classes. Here, we investigate finite-temperature magnetism in the classical 2D Heisenberg model using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations, continuously spanning the easy-plane, isotropic, and easy-axis regimes. We find that characteristic temperatures are strongly affected by the interplay of finite-size effects and spin symmetry, induced by magnetocrystalline anisotropy, being most pronounced in the isotropic Heisenberg regime and substantially reduced toward the easy-plane and easy-axis limits. Examining Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) physics in the easy-plane regime, we find that the characteristic temperatures independently estimated from the magnetization and heat capacity deviate significantly from those proposed by BKT theory, but match a previously reported anomaly in the XY-model. We report that characteristic temperatures instead are correlated with the proliferation of vortex-antivortex pairs, suggesting the gain in entropy by their accelerated nucleation as the origin of the anomaly. Establishing this connection both theoretically and numerically, we argue that the thermodynamic characteristic temperature in finite easy-plane Heisenberg magnets, including the deviation from BKT theory, is subject to the interplay between finite-size spin correlations and vortex proliferation. These results clarify how experimental and technologically relevant temperature scales evolve with magnetic anisotropy, spin symmetry, and system size in finite 2D magnets.

</details>


### [139] [Bare-Die Antiferromagnetic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33460)
*Yu Liu,Zhuoting Han,Zexin Feng,Peixin Qin,Zhiyuan Duan,Yuhao Ye,Zengwei Zhu,Chengyan Zhong,Li Liu,Guojian Zhao,Wenbin Shen,Jingyu Li,Sixu Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan,Xiaoning Wang,Ziang Meng,Chengbao Jiang,Zhiqi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种应变介导的反铁磁MnIr/PMN-PT边缘处理器，在500 K、55 T磁场与1.5 Mrad辐照下以裸片形式可靠工作，通过输入调制的原位自刷新编码实现模拟域非线性特征提取与分类，语音识别准确率99.8%、航天员视觉目标识别100%，全硬件无人机视觉系统实现太赫兹级响应与约0.2 fJ/操作能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体电子器件受量子隧穿与电荷机制制约，难以继续微缩、提升写入速度并适应恶劣环境；深空探测中的极端温度、超强磁场与强辐射会使传统电子器件迅速失效，且需大量屏蔽。现有反铁磁器件功能多局限于存储与逻辑，亟需拓展其在极端环境下的物理计算与自主智能能力。

Method: 构建应变介导的反铁磁MnIr/PMN-PT边缘处理器，利用输入调制的原位自刷新编码机制，在模拟域直接对原始信号进行非线性特征提取与分类，无需时频变换与数字预处理；并搭建全硬件集成的无人机视觉系统验证实时原位指令执行与自主导航。

Result: 器件以裸片形式在500 K、55 T磁场、1.5 Mrad辐射剂量下可靠运行；语音识别无需数字预处理即达99.8%准确率，航天员视觉目标识别达100%；无人机视觉系统实现实时原位指令执行与自主导航，响应频率达太赫兹级，能耗约0.2 fJ/操作。

Conclusion: 该研究将反铁磁器件功能从存储与逻辑拓展至模拟物理计算，证明其作为面向极端环境的节能物理计算与自主智能材料平台的潜力。

Abstract: Semiconductor electronic devices are increasingly constrained by fundamental quantum tunneling effects and charge-based mechanisms, which severely limit further miniaturization, write-speed scaling, and environmental robustness of silicon-based technologies. These limitations are particularly prohibitive for deep-space exploration, where extreme temperatures, ultra-strong magnetic fields, and intense radiation rapidly incapacitate conventional electronics without massive shielding. Here, we present an intrinsically resilient, strain-mediated antiferromagnetic MnIr/PMN-PT edge processor that operates reliably as a bare die under temperatures up to 500 K, magnetic fields of 55 T, and radiation doses of 1.5 Mrad. By exploiting an input-modulated in situ self-refreshing encoding mechanism, the device performs nonlinear feature extraction and classification directly from raw analog signals, enabling an analog computing architecture that requires no time-frequency transformation. This architecture achieves 99.8% accuracy in speech recognition without digital preprocessing and 100% accuracy in astronaut visual object recognition. Furthermore, an all-hardware integrated drone vision system demonstrates real-time in situ command execution and autonomous navigation, delivering a terahertz-level response frequency and an ultra-low energy consumption of approximately 0.2 fJ per operation. This work expands the functional scope of antiferromagnetic devices beyond memory and logic, establishing them as a promising materials platform for energy-efficient physical computing and autonomous intelligence in extreme environments.

</details>


### [140] [Topological chiral edge modes in the continuum of a trivial bulk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34000)
*Li Ge*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并揭示了一种存在于体带内部（连续谱中）的手性边缘模式（CEMICs），其由引入虚磁通（即边缘非对称耦合）所诱导，其边缘局域化机制源于能量交换引发的PT相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，拓扑物态的手性边缘模式被认为只存在于带隙内，且随晶格动量发生形变和移动。作者希望探究手性边缘模式能否存在于体带内部，即连续谱中，从而拓展对拓扑边缘态存在条件的认识。

Method: 在原本拓扑平庸的二维系统中引入虚磁通，通过沿边缘施加非对称耦合来实现。作者进一步分析CEMICs的边缘局域化来源，考察其与非厄米趋肤效应的关系，并借助系统与环境在非对称耦合结点处的能量交换来分析布里渊区中的PT相变，同时研究该系统尺寸无关的起始条件（涉及非对称耦合与几何平均之间的黄金比例）。

Result: CEMICs可以存在于体带内部（连续谱中）；较强的耦合提供优先的环绕方向；但其边缘局域化并非源于通常与非对称耦合及开边界条件相关的非厄米趋肤效应，而是源于非对称耦合结点处系统与环境之间的能量交换，并由此导致布里渊区中的PT相变；此外，CEMICs的起始在宏观极限下与系统尺寸无关，由非对称耦合与其几何平均之间的黄金比例决定。

Conclusion: 手性边缘模式不必局限于带隙内，可存在于体带（连续谱）中；其局域化机制区别于非厄米趋肤效应，由能量交换驱动的PT相变所主导，且起始条件与系统尺寸无关，由非对称耦合的黄金比例决定，为连续谱中拓扑边缘态的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Chiral edge modes in topological states of matter are routinely found inside band gaps, which may be deformed and shifted as a function of the lattice momentum. Here we show that they can exist inside a bulk band instead, i.e., in the continuum. These chiral edge modes in the continuum (CEMICs) are induced by introducing an imaginary magnetic flux to an otherwise topologically trivial two-dimensional system, realized by imposing asymmetric couplings along the edge. While the stronger couplings provide a preferred circulation direction, we find that the edge localization of CEMICs does \textit{not} originate from the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is typically associated with systems featuring asymmetric couplings and the open boundary condition. Instead, this edge localization is due to the energy exchange between the system and the environment at the asymmetric coupling junctions, which leads to a parity-time (PT) transition in the Brillouin zone. Intriguingly, the onset of CEMICs is independent of the system size in the macroscopic limit, which is given by the golden ratio between the asymmetric couplings and their geometric average.

</details>


### [141] [(Sub)nanoscale Visualization of Reconstruction-Driven Moiré Exciton Localization and Delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34104)
*Medha Dandu,Sriram Sankar,Giovanny Espitia,Rwik Dutta,Patrick J. Hays,Daria Blach,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James R. Chelikowsky,Seth Ariel Tongay,Peter Ercius,Jordan A. Hachtel,Mit H. Naik,Archana Raja,Sandhya Susarla*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过冷冻单色电子能量损失谱（EELS）结合第一性原理GW-BSE计算与光谱学，在实空间直接观测了WSe2/WS2莫尔超晶格中激子的局域化过程，揭示结构重构程度与外部应变而非新共振峰的出现才是驱动莫尔激子局域化的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱指纹（吸收与发射中的特征峰）通常被用作扭转莫尔双层中激子局域化的标志，但激子为何被限制在特定堆叠位点、其光学特征如何产生，实验上尚无定论。作者旨在从实空间直接厘清结构重构、激子局域化与光学信号之间的物理关联。

Method: 采用冷冻单色电子能量损失谱（cryogenic monochromated EELS）在实空间成像，调控WSe2/WS2莫尔超晶格中结构重构程度的变化；结合第一性原理GW-Bethe Salpeter方程（GW-BSE）计算与光学光谱进行交叉验证。

Result: 实现了莫尔激子局域化与离域化过程的实空间可视化；发现结构重构的空间范围与外部应变决定激子局域化，而光学光谱中仅出现新的莫尔激子共振峰并不足以证明激子已局域化，这与主流认识相悖。

Conclusion: 结构重构程度与外部应变是驱动莫尔激子局域化的核心因素，而非新共振峰的出现。该结论为莫尔激子工程及应变感知量子光电器件的设计提供了新原理。

Abstract: Spectral fingerprints in optical absorption and emission have typically been used as a signature of exciton localization in twisted moiré bilayers. However, the mechanism by which excitons become confined to specific stacking sites and their associated optical signature is experimentally unresolved. Here, we directly visualize in real space how tuning the change in extent of structural reconstruction leads to localization and delocalization of moiré excitons in the WSe2/WS2 moiré superlattice. Using cryogenic monochromated electron energy loss spectroscopy, together with first-principles GW-Bethe Salpeter equation calculations and optical spectroscopy, we uncover the physical mechanism that drives the correlation between twist-angle-dependent structural transformations, the real-space localization of moiré excitons, and their optical signatures. Surprisingly, and in contrast to the prevailing understanding, we show that the emergence of new moiré exciton resonances in the optical spectra alone is insufficient to establish exciton localization. Instead, the extent of structural reconstruction and external strain drives exciton localization, leading to new design principles for engineering moiré excitons and strain-aware quantum optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [142] [Observing Colossal and Tunable Near-field Thermal Radiation with A Highly Sensitive Annular Micro-thermocouple Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34355)
*Ken Araki,Wei Han Won,Liping Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用环形微热电偶结实现近场辐射传热实验测量，在10 nm间隙下实现超过黑体极限2600倍（石英）和900倍（掺杂硅）的传热增强，并借助VO2相变实现可调近场传热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场辐射传热在纳米真空间隙下可超过远场黑体极限，但室温下实现百倍以上增强通常需要亚50 nm间隙；平板构型因晶圆翘曲和污染物难以实现，而球-板构型受限于传感器制造复杂、灵敏度低和信号弱，发展不足。

Method: 在玻璃纤维末端通过薄膜沉积制备环形微热电偶结，具有25 μV/K的大塞贝克系数和8.7×10^6 K/W的热阻；在热电偶结下方附着二氧化硅微球构成球-板构型，测量不同间隙下的近场传热，并用涨落电动力学和Derjaguin近似进行建模与能量透射计算。

Result: 在10 nm间隙下观察到超黑体极限的近场辐射传热，石英增强达2600倍、掺杂硅达900倍；VO2薄膜发射极相变时在15 nm间隙实现最高430倍增强及64%的调制幅度，实验数据与理论模型吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作通过简易制备的高灵敏度微热电偶传感器克服了球-板构型近场传热实验的挑战，实现了创纪录的近场传热增强与可调谐性，将推动纳米间隙能量传输的实验研究与基础理解。

Abstract: Near-field radiative heat transfer between two objects has been theoretically predicted and experimentally demonstrated to exceed far-field blackbody limit across nanoscale vacuum gap distance enabled by evanescent surface waves coupling, while significant enhancement by more than 100 times usually requires sub-50-nm vacuum gaps around room temperature. This is challenging for parallel-plate configuration with millimeter sample sizes due to intrinsic wafer bow and contaminant particles. Sphere-plate configuration with a microsphere attached to bimaterial cantilevers or micro-thermocouple tips has been used to experimentally demonstrate near-field radiative heat transfer down to 30-nm gaps, but it is much less developed because of the challenges in the sophisticated sensor fabrication, low sensitivity and weak signals. In this work, we overcome these challenges by an annular micro-thermocouple junction fabricated at the end of a glass fiber with straightforward thin-film deposition to achieve high measurement accuracy with large Seebeck coefficient 25 uV/K and thermal resistance 8.7e6 K/W. With a silica microsphere attached underneath the micro-thermocouple junction, we report experimental observation of colossal near-field radiation heat transfer over blackbody limit down to 10-nm gap up to 2600 times with quartz and 900 times with doped silicon. Upon phase transition of VO2 thin film emitter, tunable near-field heat transfer up to 430-fold enhancement is experimentally demonstrated at 15-nm gap with 64% reduction. Experimental data agrees well with rigorous modeling based on fluctuational electrodynamics and Derjaguin approximation, and underlying mechanism is understood by energy transmission calculations. The results will advance the experimental study and fundamental understanding of energy transport at nanoscale gaps.

</details>


### [143] [Probing hydrodynamics in graphene and quark matter via Seebeck coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34544)
*Subhalaxmi Nayak,Jayanta Dey,Sabyasachi Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用动理学理论（弛豫时间近似下的玻尔兹曼方程）研究石墨烯与夸克-胶子等离子体中的塞贝克系数，发现低载流子密度（狄拉克流体）区显著偏离莫特关系，与石墨烯实验一致，并推广至夸克-胶子等离子体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将塞贝克系数作为探测集体输运行为（流体力学范畴）的探针，检验石墨烯与夸克-胶子等离子体这两类强关联量子体系中是否出现集体流体输运。

Method: 在流体力学区域采用动理学理论，基于弛豫时间近似求解玻尔兹曼输运方程以获得塞贝克系数；并将该形式推广到超相对论性的夸克-胶子等离子体。

Result: 高载流子密度（费米液体区）结果趋近常规莫特关系预期；低载流子密度（狄拉克流体区）出现对莫特关系的显著偏离，且与石墨烯实验数据吻合良好；每粒子焓在狄拉克流体区的塞贝克系数中起关键作用。推广至夸克-胶子等离子体后观察到类似的莫特关系偏离。

Conclusion: 对莫特关系的偏离表明石墨烯中出现集体流体力学输运；类似偏离在夸克-胶子等离子体中出现，支持在石墨烯与夸克物质这两个显著不同的强关联量子体系中存在相似的流体响应。

Abstract: We investigate the Seebeck coefficient as a probe of collective transport behavior in graphene and quark-gluon plasma using a kinetic-theory approach in the hydrodynamic regime. The Seebeck coefficient is obtained by solving the Boltzmann transport equation in the relaxation-time approximation. At high-charge-carrier density, corresponding to the Fermi-liquid domain, our result approaches the behavior expected from the conventional Mott relation. In contrast, significant deviations from the Mott relation are observed in the low-carrier-density regime, corresponding to the Dirac fluid domain. These facts are in good agreement with experimental Seebeck coefficient data for graphene. This behavior indicates the emergence of collective hydrodynamic transport in graphene. The enthalpy per particle plays a key role for the Seebeck coefficient in the Dirac fluid regime. We extend our formalism to the ultra-relativistic quark-gluon plasma. A similar deviation from the Mott relation is observed, supporting a similar fluid response in the Seebeck coefficient across two markedly different strongly correlated quantum systems - graphene and quark matter.

</details>


### [144] [Sub-20 nm spin textures with arbitrary topological charge stabilized by higher-order interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34706)
*Zhen Sun,Shiwei Zhu,Moritz A. Goerzen,Megha Arya,Changsheng Song,Dongzhe Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理参数化的原子自旋模型，预测Janus单层中可稳定存在尺寸小于20 nm、拓扑荷|Q|高达5的高阶磁结构（skyrmion/antiskyrmion环及skyrmion bags），并揭示高阶自旋相互作用（HOI）是其稳定化的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁结构在现代磁学中具有核心地位，同一体系中不同拓扑荷Q的共存结构在应用中极具吸引力。然而，尽管反演对称磁体中已报道了skyrmion与antiskyrmion（|Q|=1）的共存，将其拓展到任意荷的高-Q（|Q|>1）结构仍难以实现。

Method: 采用由第一性原理计算参数化的原子自旋模型，研究Janus单层（范德华磁体）中的高-Q自旋结构；分析其成核机制、热稳定性与坍缩路径。

Result: 预测了Janus单层中亚20 nm的高-Q织构，包括skyrmion/antiskyrmion环及|Q|达5的skyrmion bags。HOI（超越传统双线性交换哈密顿量）对稳定高-Q织构至关重要：环结构在零磁场下热稳定，HOI显著提升其能垒而几乎不改变尺寸，且即使无Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用也可稳定；skyrmion bags仅在存在HOI时才能成核，其中四自旋三格点相互作用是防止高-Q织构坍缩为铁磁态的关键。此外，识别出此前未报道的、依赖于宇称的高-Q织构坍缩机制。

Conclusion: HOI是一种此前被忽视的高-Q成核机制，为在Janus单层等范德华磁体中实现高拓扑荷自旋织构提供了新途径。

Abstract: Topological magnetization textures play a central role in modern magnetism. For many applications, spin textures with different topological charges $Q$ in the same system are particularly attractive. Although the coexistence of skyrmions and antiskyrmions ($|Q| = 1$) has been reported in inversion-symmetric magnets, extending this to high-$Q$ ($|Q| > 1$) textures with arbitrary charge remains elusive. Here, using an atomistic spin model parameterized from first-principles calculations, we predict the emergence of sub-20 nm high-$Q$ textures in Janus monolayers, van der Waals magnets of growing interest. We explore skyrmion and antiskyrmion rings as well as skyrmion bags with $|Q|$ up to 5, and characterize their nucleation mechanisms, thermal stability, and collapse pathways. We find that higher-order spin interactions (HOI), which extend the conventional bilinear-exchange Hamiltonian, are essential for stabilizing these high-$Q$ spin textures. The rings remain thermally stable at zero magnetic field. HOI substantially enhance their energy barriers while leaving their size nearly unchanged and stabilize them even in the absence of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. Skyrmion bags, in contrast, nucleate only in the presence of HOI. In particular, the four-spin three-site interaction is the key ingredient preventing high-$Q$ textures from collapsing into the ferromagnetic state. Finally, we identify previously unreported parity-dependent collapse mechanisms for high-$Q$ textures. Our results establish HOI as an overlooked mechanism for high-$Q$ nucleation.

</details>


### [145] [Moiré Quantum Layer Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34941)
*Jin-Xin Hu,Wen-Bo Dai,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过莫尔工程在MnBi₂Te₄薄膜中实现量子层霍尔效应（QLHE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数层拓扑反铁磁薄膜（如MnBi₂Te₄）具有层锁定贝里曲率导致的层霍尔效应（LHE），但量子化版本实验上仍难实现。

Method: 在MnBi₂Te₄薄膜表面附近引入由控制层提供静电超晶格势的莫尔控制层，且不破坏PT对称性；并通过弱栅极电场或单侧控制层调控对称性。

Result: 系统呈现净霍尔电导为零、层霍尔电导量子化为e²/h；弱电场破坏PT对称性后驱动为层极化量子反常霍尔相；单侧控制层时无需外电场即可出现层极化QAH。

Conclusion: QLHE的微观起源是标量莫尔势产生的涌现层-U(1)规范场，在顶层和底层产生相反的周期赝磁场，为反铁磁薄膜实现QLHE提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Even-layer topological antiferromagnetic thin films such as MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ exhibit an unconventional layer Hall effect (LHE) arising from a layer-locked Berry curvature. This effect manifests as a layer-polarized anomalous Hall effect that can be controlled by a vertical displacement field. However, achieving a quantized version of the LHE remains experimentally challenging. In this work, we propose that moiré engineering, using an electrostatic superlattice potential from control layers, can realize the quantum layer Hall effect (QLHE). Specifically, by placing moiré control layers in close proximity to the surfaces of a MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ thin film without breaking $PT$ symmetry, the resulting system exhibits a vanishing net Hall conductance but a quantized layer Hall conductance of $e^2/h$. A weak gate electric field then breaks $PT$ symmetry and drives the system into a layer-polarized quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) phase. Furthermore, when a single control layer is introduced on only one surface, the layer-polarized QAH emerges even in the absence of an external electric field. We identify the microscopic origin of the QLHE as an emergent layer-$U(1)$ gauge field generated by the scalar moiré potential, which in turn produces opposite periodic pseudomagnetic fields on the top and bottom layers. Our work provides a feasible pathway toward realizing the QLHE in antiferromagnetic thin films.

</details>


### [146] [Capacitance sensing in bilayer graphene with gate reflectometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35125)
*Tamás Kalmár,Gergő Fülöp,Bálint Szentpéteri,Olivér Kürtössy,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Szabolcs Csonka,Péter Makk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于栅极反射计的电容读出方法，通过LC谐振器探测hBN/WSe₂封装的双层石墨烯电容，可靠追踪位移场诱导的带隙打开，并识别hBN与BLG的小角度对准，最后提出双谐振器复用方案以提取层极化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双层石墨烯中寻找拓扑态时，电容测量广泛用于桥式读出或源漏反射计，但这些方法存在局限。作者希望发展一种替代的、可扩展的读出方法，以更好地探测范德华异质结构中的拓扑相与内部电荷动力学。

Method: 采用基于栅极反射计的读出方法，将集总元件LC谐振器耦合到异质结构顶栅电极，探测hBN与WSe₂封装的双层石墨烯薄片电容；结合固定频率微波读出与综合电容网络模型分析；并提出多路复用双谐振器方案同时测量顶栅和底栅电容。

Result: 微波读出可靠追踪位移场诱导带隙的打开；与同步DC输运测量相比，RF信号受样品无序导致的渗流输运通道影响更小；利用栅极读出识别出hBN与BLG之间的小角度对准；从固定频率读出提取了绝缘态与金属态间的器件电容变化，并用电容网络模型解释了其来源。

Conclusion: 栅极反射计是一种高度可扩展且强大的方法，适用于探索范德华异质结构中的拓扑相和内部电荷动力学，双谐振器复用方案可进一步提取体系层极化率。

Abstract: In the search for topological states in bilayer graphene, capacitance measurement methods are widely utilized in bridge type readouts or source-drain reflectometry setups. In this paper we demonstrate an alternative readout method based on gate reflectometry. We probe the capacitance of a BLG flake encapsulated between hBN and WSe$_2$ through a lumped-element LC resonator coupled to the top gate electrode of the heterostructure. The microwave readout reliably tracks the opening of a displacement field-induced band gap in the device. Compared to simultaneous DC transport measurements, the RF signal proves to be less affected by the percolating transport channels caused by sample disorder. Furthermore, we use the gate based readout to identify a small-angle alignment between the hBN and BLG flake. From the fixed frequency microwave readout, we extract the device capacitance change between the insulating and metallic state of the bilayer and utilize a comprehensive capacitance network model to explain its origin. Finally, we propose an advanced, multiplexed double-resonator readout scheme capable of simultaneously measuring top and bottom gate capacitances to extract the layer polarizability of the system. This work establishes gate reflectometry as a highly scalable and powerful methodology for exploring topological phases and internal charge dynamics in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>


### [147] [Plasmon modes in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals: tilt and gap effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35373)
*Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了三维节线半金属（无隙PTNR与有隙GNR）中的等离激元模式，取得各向异性倾斜（面内与轴向）下密度响应、Drude权重及色散的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节线半金属的等离激元行为受能隙与能带倾斜的强烈影响，但无隙与有隙情形、不同倾斜方向之间的系统对比尚不充分，需要统一框架下的解析刻画。

Method: 在低掺杂区采用线性化哈密顿量，计算无相互作用密度-密度响应函数、Drude权重，并在随机相位近似（RPA）下求解等离激元色散；处理未倾斜、沿k_x的面内倾斜与沿k_z的轴向倾斜三种构型，对部分能隙区采用小倾斜与小能隙两极限进行解析求解。

Result: 面内倾斜GNR中，足够强的倾斜或足够小的化学势使部分态密度进入部分能隙区；小能隙极限给出Drude权重与等离激元频率的分数幂修正。轴向倾斜GNR的部分能隙窗口具有双值局域费米面，得到态密度与Drude权重的闭式表达式，并分析了倾斜与质量能隙对色散及各向异性的影响。

Conclusion: 研究给出了无隙与有隙节线半金属、以及不同倾斜方向之间等离激元响应的系统对比，揭示了能隙与倾斜对Drude权重、色散及各向异性的定量影响。

Abstract: We study plasmon modes in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals, considering both the gapless $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric nodal ring (PTNR) and its gapped counterpart (GNR). Working in the low-doping regime where the linearised Hamiltonian is valid, we compute the non-interacting density-density response function, the Drude weight, and the plasmon dispersion within the random-phase approximation. We treat the untilted configuration as well as in-plane tilt along $k_x$ and axial tilt along $k_z$. For the in-plane tilted GNR, a strong-enough tilt or a small enough chemical potential pushes part of the ring into a partially-gapped regime. Only part of the Fermi surface survives there. We obtain closed-form results for this regime in two limits: a small tilt and a small gap. The small-gap limit produces a fractional-power correction to the Drude weight and the plasmon frequency. For the axially-tilted GNR, the partially-gapped window has a double-valued local Fermi surface for which we obtain the density-of-states and the Drude weights in closed forms. We analyse how the tilt and the mass-gap affect the plasmon dispersion and the anisotropy between axial and in-plane directions. Our results provide a systematic comparison between the gapless and gapped cases and between different tilt directions.

</details>


### [148] [Extracting the anyonic exchange phase from co-tunneling through an open quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35480)
*Felix Puster,Matthias Thamm,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了由三个量子点接触两两连接三段手性边缘的干涉仪,其中一段作为开放量子点,利用非平衡Keldysh微扰论计算到隧穿振幅三阶,发现干涉电流含双子准粒子交换的共隧穿贡献,其相位移动等于交换相位θ=πν,可用于提取任意子统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态的元激发服从任意子统计,其交换相位θ介于玻色与费米值之间,是拓扑量子物性的核心标志,需要可行且可解析的方案来实验提取该相位。已有反点干涉仪的共隧穿机制结构较复杂,本文力图在更简单的装置中实现同一机制。

Method: 构造三点量子点接触两两连接三段手性边缘的干涉仪,通过外加电压使其中一段成为开放量子点(谱连续、无需栅压调谐即可共振隧穿),从而在简单设置中实现反点干涉仪的共隧穿机制。采用非平衡Keldysh形式计算至隧穿振幅的三阶,求干涉电流在有限温度与量子点接触有限间距下的闭合解析表达式。

Result: 干涉电流包含双准粒子交换的共隧穿贡献,使Laughlin态的交换相位θ=πν表现为两种电压配置下Aharonov-Bohm振荡之间的相位移动。若电压引起的包围面积变化被充分屏蔽,可由稳健的相位平台差直接提取θ;若同时测量两个漏极的干涉电流,则可避免屏蔽假设,飞行时间相位可解析量化,通过受控外推以百分之几的系统误差提取θ。

Conclusion: 该三量子点接触干涉仪以简化构型实现了共隧穿任意子干涉,提供了在有限温度与实际几何条件下解析可算的干涉电流,并给出两种互补的交换相位提取方案,系统误差可达百分之几量级。

Abstract: The elementary excitations of fractional quantum Hall states obey anyonic statistics, described by an exchange phase $θ$ intermediate between the bosonic and fermionic values. We study an interferometer in which three chiral edge segments are pairwise connected by three quantum point contacts, so that, depending on the applied voltages, one of the segments acts as an open quantum dot: its spectrum is continuous, and tunneling through it is resonant without any gate tuning. This setup realizes the co-tunneling mechanism of a recently proposed antidot interferometer in a particularly simple setting. Using a non-equilibrium Keldysh calculation to third order in the tunneling amplitudes, we show that the interference current contains a co-tunneling contribution in which two quasiparticles are exchanged, so that for Laughlin states the exchange phase $θ=πν$ appears as the phase shift between the Aharonov-Bohm oscillations measured in two voltage configurations. We obtain a closed analytic expression for the interference current at finite temperature and finite separation between the quantum point contacts and show that, provided voltage-induced changes of the enclosed area are sufficiently screened, the exchange phase can be extracted from a robust phase-plateau difference. Alternatively, simultaneous measurements of the interference currents at two drains avoid this screening assumption and time-of-flight phases can be quantified analytically, allowing the exchange phase to be extracted by a controlled extrapolation with a few-percent systematic error.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamical transition in non-Hermitian Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35492)
*Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Yuan-Hang Ren,Ming Lu,Hua Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非厄米陈绝缘体中波包传播的一种特殊动力学转变：边缘波包传播不完全由陈数决定，可能因非厄米趋肤效应侵入体态，该行为源于传统拓扑局域化与非厄米趋肤效应的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在厄米陈绝缘体中，边缘波包沿边界传播，其动力学由拓扑陈数决定；而非厄米体系中广义布里渊区框架虽能预测拓扑相变，却未必能刻画波包的动力学行为。作者旨在明确非厄米陈绝缘体中边缘波包传播的实际动力学规律。

Method: 通过研究非厄米陈绝缘体中波包的传播，分析其边缘演化行为，并将其与厄米情形对比，考察传统拓扑诱导的局域化与非厄米趋肤效应之间的竞争机制，同时评估广义布里渊区框架在预测动力学转变方面的适用性。

Result: 发现边缘波包并非必然沿边界传播，而可能侵入体态：当拓扑局域化占主导时，波包沿边界演化；当非厄米趋肤效应占主导时，波包向体态扩散。广义布里渊区框架可可靠预测拓扑相变，但无法单独确定动力学转变。

Conclusion: 非厄米陈绝缘体中边缘波包传播由传统拓扑局域化与非厄米趋肤效应的竞争决定，动力学转变不能仅由陈数或广义布里渊区框架确定，需综合考量两种机制。

Abstract: We unveil a peculiar dynamical transition for the propagation of wave packets in non-Hermitian Chern insulators, where the evolution of the topological wave packets at the edge is not solely determined by the topological Chern number. Unlike the Hermitian Chern insulator, where a wave packet initiated at the edge propagates along the system boundary, here it may instead penetrate into the bulk. This behavior is attributed to the competition between the localization induced by conventional topology and the non-Hermitian skin effect. Specifically, when the former dominant, the wave packet will evolve along the boundary; otherwise, it will spread into the bulk. These features demonstrate that while the generalized-Brillouin zone framework reliably predicts topological phase transitions, it does not on its own specify the dynamical transitions.

</details>


### [150] [Superfluid Spin Transport in the Van der Waals Antiferromagnet CrCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35513)
*Peisen Yuan,Xiaomin Guo,Vincent Flynn,Benedetta Flebus,Fèlix Casanova,Luis E. Hueso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文在易平面范德华反铁磁绝缘体CrCl₃中通过非局域器件结构发现并调控了超流自旋输运，表现为约90 μm的超长程弱衰减传输，并证实强磁场与高温可抑制该超流态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流自旋输运因可实现相位梯度驱动、超低损耗的自旋输运而成为自旋电子学前沿，但实验研究受限于缺乏合适材料体系，进展缓慢。

Method: 采用非局域器件结构，结合非局域磁子（magnon）输运测量与理论建模，在易平面范德华反铁磁绝缘体CrCl₃中研究自旋输运行为。

Result: 在倾斜（canted）反铁磁自旋构型下，CrCl₃中出现超流自旋输运，呈现约90 μm的超长程、弱衰减自旋传输；强磁场和高温会抑制超流态，使输运恢复为随距离快速指数衰减的非相干磁子行为。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了二维磁绝缘体中超流自旋输运的潜力，并确立CrCl₃作为下一代自旋电子学中高能效、长距离自旋输运的候选平台。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the quest for spin superfluidity has moved to the forefront of spintronics, driven by the promise of phase-gradient-driven, ultra-low-loss spin transport. However, experimental investigations remain limited, primarily due to the lack of suitable material systems. Here, we report on the discovery and control of a superfluid spin transport in the easy-plane van der Waals antiferromagnetic (AFM) insulator CrCl$_3$ by a nonlocal device structure. Combining nonlocal magnon transport measurements with theoretical modelling, we demonstrate that spin superfluidity emerges in CrCl$_3$ under canted AFM spin configurations, where it gives rise to ultra-long range (around 90 $μ$m), weakly decaying spin transport. We also provide direct evidence that strong magnetic fields and elevated temperatures suppress the superfluid state, restoring the rapid exponential decay with distance of incoherent magnons. These findings underscore the potential of spin superfluidity in two-dimensional magnetic insulators and establish CrCl$_3$ as a promising platform for energy-efficient, long-distance spin transport in next-generation spintronic applications.

</details>
